## Release: v0.8.2
### Release Date: 2026-10-20
#### ⚠️ Breaking Changes:

#### 🌟 New Features:
- **NSA vs SA in Scoring & GAP Analysis:** with NSA and SA CDRs in the workspace, **NR Mode** also offers **NSA vs SA**, which scores the NSA and SA CDRs of the chosen campaigns together so the scorings, KPI tables, GAP analysis and Scoring Trends show the NSA and SA results of each operator side by side, and every results tab shows its tables and charts again for each NR Mode on its own (such as the Best Network Scoring Trend and the National & Geographical Areas Trends of the NSA campaigns and of the SA ones), each panel of NSA right above the same panel of SA with their filters once above both or, with **NSA vs SA** set to All NSA, then all SA, every panel of NSA and then every panel of SA with their own filters; **NSA & SA charts** puts NSA and SA in the same Scoring Charts and Scoring Trends by default (one line per operator and NR Mode, SA dashed, the NSA and SA campaigns of a quarter on one point) or in separate ones, NSA and SA keep their colours in every tab and theme, and How the measurements ran of both NR Modes comes first, whose Campaigns shows the latest NSA and SA campaigns in large and the others below, with the tests of each NR Mode. Each campaign keeps the NR Mode of its CDRs (an SA campaign that does not name SA gets it, also in the Daily to Yearly periods), so the NSA and SA tests of an operator are never added together even when both CDRs use the same operator names; the calculation is always split by Campaign, Latest campaign and the other automatic choices count quarters (Latest campaign compares 2026-Q3 NSA with 2026-Q3 SA), and each NR Mode is scored and compared with the GAP reference on its own, so the NSA and SA figures are those of an NSA and an SA calculation. Its **GAP reference operator** can also be **Same operator in NSA**, which gives the GAP of each operator in SA to itself in the NSA campaign of the same quarter (or period), KPI by KPI and in total, on the page and in PowerPoint and Word. Saved Filters, their export, import, transfers and backups keep it, the Scoring artifacts of the Reporting Jobs offer it in their NR Mode and the Scoring section of the Bundle Report Templates as **Compare NSA vs SA**. The PowerPoint and Word show each NR Mode apart as the results do (each slide of one NR Mode tagged NSA or SA, the SA trend lines dashed in the same chart): Export Selected Scoring Report and Generate Scoring Report with the NSA vs SA and NSA & SA charts chosen on the page, and the Scoring artifacts and the Scoring section with their own **NSA vs SA** and **NSA & SA charts**. The documents and file names read NSA vs SA.
- **Excluded Calls in Scoring & GAP Analysis:** the **Excluded Calls** panel of Scoring & GAP Analysis keeps lists of calls left out of every scoring (Saved Filters with their Geographical Areas, Scoring Trends and Points Lost, scoring reports, Reporting Jobs and Bundle Report Templates), imported by their NetCheck **JOIN_ID** from a CSV, TXT or Excel file (a JOIN_ID, JOINT_ID or Call Key column and an optional Reason column, or a single column of IDs), with a template to download. Each list has a name and reason, applies to every CDR or to the CDRs chosen (a Voice or Speech CDR brings the Speech or Voice CDR of the same campaign and NR Mode, whose samples belong to the same calls), and is switched on or off, edited, downloaded or deleted by those who edit the Scoring Methodologies. The calls are found in the CDRs in the background and the panel shows how many calls and rows of each list each CDR holds, with the JOIN_IDs found in none of them to download. A list changed recalculates only the scorings of the CDRs it touches (the Scoring History indexes them again without the calls), and each result names in its warnings, and in the notes of its slides, the lists and the calls and CDR rows they leave out. Every action is recorded in App Events, Database Management lists the tables **Scoring Exclusion Lists**, **Scoring Excluded Calls** and **Scoring Exclusion Matches**, and the lists travel as the **Scoring Excluded Calls** element of Import / Export / Transfer, with Full Workspace transfers and with backups; the panel is listed after Scoring Calculation in the floating section bar and in the sections menus of the module (its tab and the side menu), which open it.
- **Comments of the Operator names:** the **Operator names found in the CDRs** table of the Operator Maps has a **Comments** column, after Role and wider than the others, to note what each name holds in the CDRs (for example the SA terminal of an NSA drive), and its Label and Comments fields are pastel yellow; **Save assignments** saves the comments, every change is recorded in App Events, and they travel with the Operator Maps in the Mappings & Reference Data export, transfers and backups.
- **Scoring charts and tables as images:** every card of the Scoring & GAP Analysis results (a chart, map, table or panel) shows a blue floating download button at its top right corner while the pointer is over it (a tap on touch screens), which stays in its place while the pointer moves over the card and saves the whole card, and, in a card that holds several charts or tables, the one under the pointer shows a light blue one of its own that saves only it, each as a PNG image as it is shown, in its theme and with every column of a table that scrolls sideways and every row of one that scrolls downwards or is taller than the window; beside each download, a screenshot button copies the same image to the clipboard to paste it elsewhere, and App Events records each download and copy.
- **Default values in the NQ Analysis Configuration:** every configuration of the Non-Qualified Calls Analysis Center can go back to the values of a new workspace: **Default Columns** in Columns (no optional or CDR columns, and the Analysis Center fields of the default catalog), **Default Fields & Sections** in Fields & Sections and **Default Catalog** in the Root Cause Catalog fill their dialog and **Save** applies them (a value still used by a call is never removed), beside Default Statuses, Default Teams, Default Rules and Default Rule, and **Default Equivalences** in Previous NQ Analyses forgets, once confirmed, the equivalences uploaded for the workspace; App Events records each one.

#### 🚀 Enhancements:
- **Merges in the Operator Maps:** the **Merges N** status of an Operator name, the **Merges N** badge of a label and the confirmation when saving count only the names of a label that coincide in the same campaign and NR Mode, which are the ones added up as one, and say where they coincide (for example `26-Q2 (NSA)`); names that share a label but are never in the same campaign and NR Mode (such as `Vodafone UK` in NSA and `Vodafone SM` in an SA campaign) stay **Assigned**, and hovering it lists them.
- **Users count:** the count of the users of Admin → Users, or of those listed by its search and filters out of them, is a badge beside **Collapse** instead of text after the filters.
- **Modules of the Bundle Report Templates editor:** the **Sections** of the editor are called **Modules**, and below its Campaigns each module shows its **Options** and then its **Sub-modules** (its Dashboards in PPT Dashboards), each in a subpanel of its own, and the **Favourite** list is as wide as its longest name.
- **Enable/Disable in Reporting Jobs:** the jobs table has an **Enable/Disable** switch on each job, before its actions, which enables or disables it without editing it (in place of the ⏸/⏵ action), recorded in App Events.
- **Renamed favourites rename their jobs:** renaming a favourite Bundle Report Template renames every Reporting Job based on it, also one that was given another name, and the Rename dialog lists them.
- **Scoring charts without horizontal scroll:** the Scoring per Service, per Category and per Category (Breakdown) charts of Scoring & GAP Analysis always show every bar in the width of their card, at the height they have with ten bars, so no City or other first level of the Split by is cut: with more bars the bars are narrower, their totals smaller or turned 90°, the labels inside them a little smaller than the totals (turned too when needed), and the labels of each level under them at one size, in up to two lines, or all turned 90° in a taller row when one does not fit across.
- **Folded Favourites Filters with Use Filter:** the cards of the **Favourites Filters** panel and of the **Scoring Results** dropdown of Scoring & GAP Analysis are folded, showing only their name (their Filter ID when they have none) and the line of their filters; clicking a card unfolds the rest of its details and its buttons, and **Use Filter**, on the right of its name, makes it the Saved Filter selected at the top of the module and opens its Scoring. A card whose Saved Filter filters a single Region, Cluster, City, Operator or Vendor field has a label on top with its values (for example **London** or **Vendor A**), or **All** when it filters none of them, in the colour of the field, beside an outlined tab with its NR Mode. The selected card at the top of the module is folded too, showing its name, the line of its filters and its buttons, with **Details** to unfold the rest, and the same Saved Filter selector is also on top of the Calculation panel, beside its title, with a blue **Tip** card on its left on where to start (a Saved Filter or Calculate Scoring); the selected card has the label of its Saved Filter on top too, the counters read **N Favourites Filters**, and the list of both dropdowns scrolls on its own without scrolling the page.
- **Result tabs always in view:** while the results of Scoring & GAP Analysis are scrolled, their tab bar (Summary to Points Lost) stays at the top of the window, below the bar of the sections, on a band that hides the page behind it, until the end of the results; a tab chosen there opens at its start, and the button that downloads a chart or table as an image stays below the bar.
- **Warning in the Status Rules:** the **Status Rules** dialog of the Non-Qualified Calls Analysis Center shows an amber warning card above the rules: modifying them without the necessary knowledge can completely change how the calls are classified, since saving them gives every call with an automatic status the status of the new rules.
- **CDRs used in Scoring & GAP Analysis:** each type (Data, Voice and Speech) shows four CDRs before it scrolls, whatever lines their names take.

#### 🐛 Bug fixes:
- **Campaign Comparison across methodologies:** when the two campaigns compared were scored with different methodologies, a category whose KPIs changed between them (a KPI added or removed, or other maximum points, such as VIDEO STREAM when 2026-Q3 shares the YouTube points with TikTok) is compared as a whole, on the page and in PowerPoint and Word: one row with the points of all its KPIs in each campaign and their Δ Points, its KPIs below it without a Δ of their own, and a note naming the category and the methodology of each campaign. The KPIs whose maximum points shrank no longer show losses that are not there, the new KPIs count in the Scoring Points Gap, which adds up the Δ Points shown, and the other categories are still compared KPI by KPI.
- **Order of the Geographical Areas levels:** the cards and the rankings with their comparisons with the reference go from the largest level to the smallest (Region, Cluster, City, after the scoring Nationwide), on the page, whose rankings open on the largest level, and in PowerPoint and Word, which export every level instead of the Regions coming after every slide of the cities.
- **Buttons of Scoring & GAP Analysis:** Generate Scoring Report and Export Selected Scoring Report are as tall as the buttons beside them and aligned with them, without the rule above them that a style of Workspace Config added, and the buttons of Scoring & GAP Results are as tall as those of the Calculation panel.
- **Campaigns of the Bundle Report Templates sections:** on narrow screens, such as a phone, the Campaigns of each section and, in Scoring & GAP Analysis, the GAP reference and Methodology beside them are shown one below the other instead of being squeezed out of sight.
- **Scoring charts per City:** with City (or Region or Cluster) above Operator in the aggregation hierarchy, the Scoring per Service and per Category charts leave their gap between the cities instead of between the operators, and their PowerPoint and Word slides, when split, keep every operator and campaign of each city together instead of each operator across all the cities; the Split by note and the Aggregation hierarchy dialog say that the levels above Operator group the operators.
- **Open lists above the next panel:** the lists of the multiple choices, such as the Campaigns of the Scoring Trends, and those of the Reporting pickers open above the panel that follows theirs instead of under it.
- **Reloading Scoring & GAP Analysis:** a reload (or Back and Forward) opens the page where it was being read, showing what was in view when it was left, dimmed under a gradient with a green loading notice, until its results are drawn there, instead of showing the top of the page first and then moving down; opening the module from its tab or after signing in starts at its top instead of going to the end of the page.

#### 📚 Documentation:

## Release: v0.8.1
### Release Date: 2026-10-19
#### ⚠️ Breaking Changes:
- **Ticket Center packages:** Import / Export / Transfer and Backup Protection move the tickets as the **Ticket Center** element (`ticket-center` packages); the Bugs Report packages and the Bugs Report element of the backups of earlier versions no longer import. The tickets of the server, the module in the Modules Profiles and Interface Settings, the notices told to the super-admins, the scheduled backup selection and the App Events of Bugs Report take their Ticket Center names in place.

#### 🌟 New Features:
- **Scoring Export Panels:** Workspace Config lists every panel of the Scoring & GAP Analysis results that the PowerPoint and Word can include, in a card per results tab with its colour and icon, with an **Exportable** switch and **What to Export** buttons (**All Operators** or **Focus Operators**, the default marked with a dot) and **Restore Defaults**. Panels that are not exportable are left out of Export Selected Scoring Report, Generate Scoring Report, the Scoring artifacts of Reporting Jobs and the Scoring section of the Bundle Report Templates, whose editors list only the exportable ones (those of the Focus operators tagged **Focus**); **Export All Operators in Focus panels**, in the export dialog of a Saved Filter, the report editor (saved with its configurations), the Scoring artifacts and the Bundle Report Templates, shows every operator in the Focus Operators panels of a document. Changes are recorded in App Events, and the panels travel as the **Scoring Export Panels** element of Import / Export / Transfer, with Full Workspace transfers and with backups.
- **Ticket Center:** Bugs Report is now **Ticket Center**, with a ticket icon, at `/tickets` (the `/bugs-report` addresses, bookmarks and notices of before lead there, and a stored Bugs Report title, short title and icon of Interface Settings, and the Bugs Report of the Non-Qualified Calls profile description, become Ticket Center once). Every ticket has a type, **Bug**, **Improvement** or **New Feature**, each with its colour and icon badge: the New Ticket form asks for it (and shows the description guide of the type), super-admins change it in **Manage** (recorded in the timeline and App Events), the tickets of earlier versions are bugs, and it travels with the tickets in export, import, transfer, backup and restore. The header is a dashboard: tiles of the open, New, In Progress and Waiting for Info tickets, those resolved in the last 30 days and my open ones; a Type × Status matrix with totals; a card per type with its tickets by status and its open ones by priority; the open tickets per module; the tickets created and resolved in each of the last 12 weeks and the age of the oldest open ticket. Every count lists its tickets, and the list has a Type column and chips to filter by type, status, priority, module and assignee, besides the search, reporter and order. The super-admin notices name the type of each new ticket, the App Events record *Ticket created*, *Ticket managed*… and the Database Viewer lists the tables **Tickets**, **Ticket Comments** and **Ticket Attachments**.

#### 🚀 Enhancements:
- **Focus and excluded operators in the Operator Maps:** each Operator label of Workspace Config → Operator Maps has a **Role** beside it: **Include** (yellow, the default), **Focus** (green) or **Exclude** (red), also shown read only beside each name found in the CDRs. **Focus** labels (with every name of the CDRs they stand for) are the Focus operators of the Scoring & GAP Analysis PowerPoint and Word (those of the panels set to Focus Operators in Scoring Export Panels: by default Where to act, the Biggest levers, the places and locations ranked, the individual GAP comparisons, GAP by part, the KPI GAP Profile, the Points Lost views and the Campaign Comparison) and come first, under **Focus operators** and before the **Other operators**, in the operator choices of those sections of the page, replacing the Focus operators field of the Calculation panel, the Saved Filter cards, the Scoring artifacts of the Reporting Jobs and the Scoring section of the Bundle Report Templates (the operators those and the report scenarios kept are no longer saved, exported or imported). **Exclude** leaves an operator out of every analysis, filter, chart and report (Scoring & GAP Analysis, CDR Analysis, PPT Dashboards, the Chart Builder, Network Insights, Non-Qualified Calls, the Bundle Report Templates and the Reporting Jobs) and of every Operator list and GAP reference, so the Operator filters can stay at All; at least one label always stays included. The roles are recorded in App Events and travel with the Operator Maps in exports, transfers and backups, and the shipped favourites, Favourites Filters and Reporting Jobs choose every operator.
- **Latest 3 and Latest 4 campaigns:** every Campaign list that offers the automatic choices (Scoring & GAP Analysis and its Scoring Trends, PPT Dashboards, Network Insights, the report scenarios, the Bundle Report Templates and the Reporting Jobs) offers them in this order: Latest campaign, Latest 2 campaigns, Latest 3 campaigns, Latest 4 campaigns, Previous campaign and All available campaigns; like the others, they follow the newest campaigns each time they are used.
- **Delete the checked Favourites Filters:** beside **Use newer CDRs**, **Delete** (with how many are checked) deletes every Saved Filter checked in the Favourites Filters panel and its result after one confirmation, keeping their CDRs and the Scoring History; each one is recorded in App Events.
- **Results of a selection that is no Saved Filter:** when the filters of the Calculation panel are not those of any Saved Filter (for example after changing the Campaign of an opened one), the Scoring Results dropdown says so and asks to choose a Favourites Filter or to calculate the selection, instead of the card, the results and the export of the Saved Filter opened before, which come back as soon as the selection is a Saved Filter again or one is opened or calculated.
- **Duplicate a Saved Filter:** **⧉** on each Saved Filter card asks for the name of a copy and opens its filters in the edit dialog; **Save as New Filter** saves the changed selection (for example another Campaign) as a new Favourites Filter right after the copied one, which stays as it was, and calculates it. It is recorded in App Events with the Saved Filter copied.
- **Close Scoring:** beside **Recalculate**, **Close Scoring** closes the scoring shown in Scoring & GAP Results (such as the last one calculated, unstarred), keeping its Saved Filter: its card and results give way to *No scoring is open* and none opens on its own, also after reloading the page, until a Saved Filter is opened or calculated. Opening a Saved Filter from its card and closing it are recorded in App Events.
- **Operator choices stand out in Scoring:** the Operator filter of the Calculation panel and the operator choices of the results (Where to act, Route stretches, Campaign Comparison and the GAP comparisons) are shown in the blue of the Operators, stronger than the other fields, also with the Midnight theme.
- **Operator buttons of Scoring go together:** every section with operator buttons starts with its Focus operators enabled (every operator without them), shows the Focus operators and the others in two subpanels side by side after its Operators label, and follows the others: after a click in one section, every section (also those drawn later) shows its operators as that one does; the PowerPoint and Word show the operators chosen on the page and, without a choice, those of each panel in Scoring Export Panels (the Focus operators or every operator), the reference included when it is one of them; the route that loses most, the individual GAP comparisons and the Campaign Comparison always show those of their panel, and the Points Lost Map follows the operators of the KPI GAP Profile.
- **Layout of the Scoring results:** sections with four operators show them two above and two below, the tables of the Geographical Areas name the level chosen (Region, Cluster or City, cities and routes only when the City level has Connecting Roads routes) instead of Location, on the page and in PowerPoint and Word, whose rankings and comparisons with the reference (*<operator> against <reference> per <city>*) cover every level and group of the results, the Cities, Towns, Roads and Railways ranked (a table per operator) and the route that loses most show every operator chosen with operator buttons of their own, whatever the operator of Where to act, GAP by part shows two operators a row, Connecting Roads: where along each route the points are shows every chosen operator at once, Where to act and the Campaign Comparison choose their operator with buttons in the Focus and Other operators subpanels, the Scoring and GAP tables with many columns (such as four operators of four campaigns) fit without scrolling sideways, and the Points Lost Map has Maps per and Colour areas by below its location, the operators beside them.
- **National points in the Points Lost views:** the Points Lost Map and Where to act, by performance owner, on the page and in PowerPoint and Word, say that their figures are National points (the part of the National loss in the tests of each place, adding up to it; the map column and the owner cards read **National points lost**), not the score of each place on its own tests, which the National & Geographical Areas Trends show.
- **Scoring Nationwide in Geographical Areas:** the tab opens with the scoring of the whole country (also for a Saved Filter with Region, Cluster or City filters), on a card across the panel with larger bars and text, before the cards of each area, on the page and in PowerPoint and Word; Saved Filters calculated before show it from their own calculation or that of their National & Geographical Areas Trends. It is exported with the cards of the areas, an option now called **Scoring Nationwide and per City / Cluster / Region cards** in the report editor, the Scoring Export Panels and the Bundle Report Templates.

#### 🐛 Bug fixes:
- **Wide Scoring charts:** a chart with more bars than fit its card (such as three campaigns of four operators) is fitted to the card keeping its height (up to 16 bars, such as four campaigns of four operators, are drawn narrower in the card, with smaller totals and the labels of their parts smaller or turned 90°), and otherwise scrolls horizontally with a zoom out that narrows it down to the width of the card (the whole chart, its bars narrower but as tall, its texts unchanged) and a zoom in that widens it back; the larger view (double-click) always shows the whole chart ([Bug report #2](https://108.132.147.165:8443/bugs-report/2)).
- **Campaigns in the Scoring Summary:** the Campaigns card of how the measurements ran (on the page, in PowerPoint and in Word) names the reference campaign also when Campaign is not in the Split by, instead of a dash, and below it how many other campaigns were analysed and which, the newest first (such as +2 campaigns: 26-Q2, 26-Q1; One campaign without others), and the tests of each operator name their campaign only when they are those of one campaign.
- **Calculate, Open and Recalculate stay disabled:** after changing the selection of the Calculation panel while a Saved Filter is calculating (for example the Campaign of an opened Saved Filter), the buttons are enabled again: checking whether the selection is already a Saved Filter answers about ten times faster in workspaces with many CDRs, and the refresh of the Favourites Filters list every few seconds no longer discards a check still on its way.
- **Operators missing from Sites to review:** an operator none of whose serving cells is in the cell inventories (so no site of it can be reviewed) is no longer left out without a word: a note names it with its points lost that cannot be assigned to sites.
- **Scoring results with the Midnight theme:** the labels on the bars and bands keep the colour chosen against their fill, the totals over the bars have a dark outline, the heading of each results tab stays visible on the light page, and the operator buttons keep their colours (a lighter outline, so a black operator stays visible, and a faint fill for those not chosen).
- **Renamed Operator and Vendor labels in every saved filter:** renaming a label in the Operator Maps or Vendor Maps also renames it in the Saved Filters of Scoring & GAP Analysis (their GAP reference and filters, so they are found again from the Calculation panel instead of saying that no Saved Filter matches and hiding their calculation and results) and in the favourite Bundle Report Templates and Network Insights Analysis Selections, besides the Reporting Jobs, report configurations, Dashboards, Report Templates and the other saved filters.
- **Notes above the Geographical Areas cards:** the note above the cards of each area keeps its own room, instead of the top rule of the cards crossing its text.

#### 📚 Documentation:

---

## Release: v0.8.0
### Release Date: 2026-10-19
#### ⚠️ Breaking Changes:

#### 🌟 New Features:
- **Summary, Geographical Areas, Scoring Trends and Points Lost in Scoring & GAP Analysis:** the results have seven tabs, opening on **Summary**: how the measurements ran (campaigns, days, places and tests), where to act for the operator chosen (the themes that hold its points lost with what to do and the Points Lost views that show it, each a link to its section, each KPI and stretch with the points of the scoring it loses against its maximum, every figure of points in bold in the loss colour of the theme) and a card per performance owner (the Regions of the CDRs, such as Vendor A, Vendor B, VMO2 North and VMO2 South) with its points, main issue, biggest areas, High priority sites and where its points are, then where each operator stands (its score, position, GAP to the reference by part and the operators around it), its position in every environment and service, the biggest levers against the reference and, for the operator chosen in Where to act, its Cities, Towns, Roads and Railways ranked with their owners, change, Voice and Data shares and points lost, and the route that loses most with its stretches on a map drawn as the Connecting Roads ones with each circle ringed in the colour of its owner, the points of each owner and its five stretches with the most points, all also as slides of PowerPoint and Word; **Geographical Areas** gathers the scoring of each area of the **Breakdown** (Region by default, Cluster, City or None) and of each of the **Separate cities** (London by default; **Main Cities** is kept as such and stands for the Main Cities of the workspace at each calculation) of the **Geographical Areas & Scoring Trends** panel of the calculation, whatever its Split by: their cards, every location ranked and each operator against the reference per location (Saved Filters calculated before are calculated again when opened); **Scoring Trends** shows the Scoring Trend (from two campaigns on) and the National & Geographical Areas Trends of those areas (National, then the Regions or Clusters, then the cities, each group in its colour with a thick rule between groups, also on the slides), calculated with the campaigns of the **Scoring Trends** subpanel (**All available campaigns** by default, Latest 2 campaigns… whatever the Campaign filter of the rest of the scoring, which they follow without campaigns of their own) and its time split. The panel is always included, with its Geographical Areas and Scoring Trends side by side, and both tabs are always scored on the whole country, also with Region, Cluster or City filters; the report scenarios and the Bundle Report Templates have the same panel (with Main Cities) and the Saved Filter cards show the Split by, the GAP reference with the Focus operators (kept with each Saved Filter, also when it is calculated again, and renamed with the Operator Maps), the methodology, the Geographical Areas and the Scoring Trends, and the GAP reference operator, the **Focus operators** (operators, or patterns such as `VF*` or `NOT Lebara` typed in their filter, see *Operator patterns*; every operator without them, or when none of the results is one of them: the operators of Where to act and Where to act, by performance owner with their places and route, the locations ranked and per location, the individual GAP comparisons, GAP by part, the KPI GAP Profile and the Points Lost views of the PowerPoint and Word, every Focus operator in Where to act; on the page every operator stays available, the Focus operators first, under Focus and a line, and chosen by default in those sections; in Scoring artifacts for every scenario of the report) and the methodology sit in this order beside the Campaign of the calculation, of the Scoring artifacts and of the Scoring section of the Bundle Report Templates; **GAP Analysis** adds the GAP by part of the scoring over the campaigns; and **Points Lost** shows where the points go (failed tests, below the threshold, quality shortfall), the points lost per KPI, the Points Lost Map and the points lost per Vendor and kind of serving site, the sites to review with their priority and where along each Connecting Roads route the points are (a map of the route on the map areas with the measured cities around it always named, each stretch sized and coloured from yellow to red by its points lost and the numbered ones named after their nearest city, as in the Route stretches slides; on both route maps, choosing a stretch in the table beside the map zooms the map in on it, and double-clicking the map opens it larger). The report editor, PowerPoint and Word follow the same groups and order (configurations saved before keep their choices); the views of the serving cells and the test counts need the Saved Filter calculated again.
- **Themes and scorecard tables for Scoring & GAP Analysis:** a **Theme** selector at the start of the floating section bar styles every chart and table of the results and the PowerPoint and Word exports: **NetCheck** (as the NetCheck reports: white lists with Excel-like data bars of each score's share of the maximum), **Umlaut** (as the umlaut benchmark reports: bold dark titles over a red headline, bars in the colour of each band), **Ericsson** (the default, for every user who has chosen none and for new reports: the colours of the Ericsson brand guide: its greys, table headers in its deep blue (the scorecards headed in white, each operator in dark ink over the rule of its colour), light tints for the fills and deep shades for the ends of each scale; cells filled as far as that share, GAPs with a ▲ or ▼ in their colour over a bar), **Vodafone** (pills), **Midnight** (the only dark theme, glowing cards; its exported content slides are night blue with the logos in white and every table, card, panel and chart on them dark), **Paper** (typeset) and **Heatmap** (light heat tiles). The Scoring and GAP tables read as a scorecard on the page and in the exports: sections per category with their subtotal, each KPI with its R/D type, weight and maximum instead of the Category, Type, Weight and Max columns, operators in their colour (in Ericsson ruled across all their columns), each group below the operators (a route, city or campaign) ruled under its name and set apart from the next one by a gap on the page and a line in the exports, the best and worst operators highlighted inside their box and every GAP a bar either side of zero (red to the left, green to the right). The National & Geographical Areas table, the scale of the Points Lost Map and the maximum score donuts (each ring in colours of its own) change with the theme too, and each theme gives the titles and subtitles of every content slide its own font and style (NetCheck in Montserrat with its first part in light capitals, Ericsson in black Ericsson Hilda Light with only the part that changes from one slide to the next in bold Ericsson Hilda and the deep Ericsson blue, and grey subtitles, Vodafone in Vodafone Rg, Paper in Georgia…). Each user keeps one theme per workspace for every themed module (Scoring & GAP Analysis, Network Insights, CDR Analysis and PPT Dashboards: choosing it in one chooses it in all of them, registered in App Events) chosen at the start of the floating section bar of each of them, the report editor has its own Theme saved with each configuration, and operator colours still come from the Operator Maps.
- **Bundle Report Templates in Reporting:** the module is called **Reporting** (the Scheduled Reporting title kept by Interface Settings and the Non-Qualified Calls profile becomes Reporting once) and opens with a **Bundle Report Templates** panel that builds one PowerPoint or Word document (a bundle report, kept in `output/reports/bundle-reports/`) from PPT Dashboards, Scoring & GAP Analysis (Summary, Scoring Charts, Scoring Tables, Locations, Scoring Trends, GAP Analysis and Points Lost), Network Insights and Non-Qualified Calls (whole panels or some of their charts and tables), with common filters as in Scoring (NR Mode, Operators, Vendors and area, which replace the saved filters of the Dashboards), the Campaigns of each section (which choose its CDRs), the options of each section (scorings, environments, methodology, GAP reference and KPI and GAP values of Scoring; technology, Split by and thresholds of Network Insights; periods of Non-Qualified Calls), one **Theme** (in violet) beside the title and subtitle, the only theme the report keeps, read by every section that supports themes (Scoring, Network Insights and PPT Dashboards), and the sections and sub-modules ordered by dragging; the Campaigns of each section stand out in amber. The document opens with a cover with a large title, an optional subtitle and every common filter, and each section with its own cover naming its Campaigns, the common filters and its options (full pages in Word). **PowerPoint** and **Word** preview every slide or page before the download: a chart or table keeps to one slide or page while it stays legible (a table spread over several slides becomes one, with the columns of its parts joined and a long table continued in tables side by side; it goes on to more slides only when its values would be smaller than 6 pt, 6 pt on the page in Word, tables side by side such as one per network going on together, and the pages of other charts and tables go on when they would be shown below 60 % of their size; small components share a slide, each under a caption that does not repeat the slide title), and each Word page holds as many components of a section as fit, in their order, a part, a view (such as the Points Lost per City of every operator), a component or its pages that does not fit whole in what is left of a page starting the next one; the Word cover is followed by a Contents page in the fonts and colours of the report theme with each section, part and view, its page and a link to it. Reports are saved as favourites, travel as the Bundle Report Template Favourites element of Import / Export / Transfer, with Full Workspace transfers and with backups (Reporting Jobs packages made before, which carried them, still restore them), and can be an artifact of a Reporting Job. The preview takes almost the whole screen, with every section and page to go to and the page zoomed with the wheel, a rectangle or its buttons. Each section generated is used again by the next exports while its data has not changed (the other format of the same report only composes it), and an export that repeats an earlier one also uses its composed document, packed Word pages and preview, and Scoring uses the Saved Filter already calculated for the same CDRs, filters, methodology and GAP reference (a starred one first); the sections are by default Scoring & GAP Analysis, Network Insights, Non-Qualified Calls and PPT Dashboards, in this order (a saved favourite keeps its own).
- **Favourite Analysis Selections in Network Insights:** the Analysis Selection panel saves the selection as a favourite with a name, to choose it again and analyse it (at once when its analysis is stored; the selector keeps showing it after a reload), rename it keeping the selection it saved, or delete it; favourites belong to the workspace and travel with the Network Insights Favourites element of Import / Export / Transfer, Full Workspace transfers and backups.
- **Key Findings in Network Insights:** a panel after the Overview with six benchmark views of the CDR Data tests, one value per test: the LTE and 5G signal at the test (RSRP and SINR classes per environment), the spread of the total bandwidth of the downloads and their share under 40 MHz, how often 5G was used (drive and walk apart, from `G_Level_1`, with the share of each test's time on 5G), the main 4G band and 5G on 3.5 GHz, and the total bandwidth ladder. Each one is drawn as a slide with a headline and a **What it means** written from its figures, the tests it covers, its basis and a footnote with its scope and Campaign (the latest one with a split by Campaign), and PowerPoint, Word, Bundle Report Templates and Reporting Jobs show them as slides drawn with editable shapes.
- **Themes in CDR Analysis and PPT Dashboards:** a **Theme** selector at the start of the floating section bar of each page applies the themes of Scoring & GAP Analysis: CDR Analysis colours its page charts and its PowerPoint and Word exports (chart cards, CDF curves and bars, metric cards, table headers and rows, content slide titles in the theme's title style), PPT Dashboards its viewer and its Dashboard PPTs (the font and the colours of text, axes, grid lines, table headers and rows of the charts, keeping the operator, vendor and threshold colours, and the slide titles in the theme's title style); the page of CDR Analysis follows it at once in its cards, statistic tiles, tables, labels and accents, and the selector of PPT Dashboards also sits in the floating bar of the page; one theme per user per workspace shared with the other themed modules, recorded in the App Events log.
- **Operator patterns:** every Operator filter (Scoring & GAP Analysis and its report scenarios, CDR Analysis, PPT Dashboards, Network Insights, Non-Qualified Calls, the Bundle Report Templates and the Reporting Jobs) and the Focus operators take patterns typed in their list and saved as such, resolved each time they are used: `*` for any characters (`VF*` takes every VF variant, also those that appear later) and `-` or `NOT` before a value to leave it out (`NOT Lebara` is every operator but Lebara, several exclusions leave out each one, `VF*` with `-VF SM` every VF variant but VF SM); they come first in their list, take the spellings of the Operator Maps and travel with exports, transfers and backups.
- **Scoring report of the page:** the report editor that **Generate Scoring Report** opens only chooses the content of one report (scorings, environments and sections), without saved configurations, theme or JSON files: the report takes the filters, Split by, Geographical Areas & Scoring Trends, GAP reference, Focus operators, methodology and theme of the page.
- **Open Scoring:** when the selection of the Calculation panel is a Saved Filter with its result up to date, **Calculate Scoring** reads **Open Scoring**, with a folder open, since it only opens it.

#### 🚀 Enhancements:
- **Scoring KPI GAP Profile, location cards and trend:** the KPI GAP Profile shows the GAP to the reference per KPI grouped by category with subtotals, two operators side by side (the points lost per KPI are in Points Lost), its column headers aligned like their values as in every insight table, and both tables mark each KPI with the R or D badge of its type, as the Score and GAP tables do, in two clearly different colours in every theme; the location cards order the operators from the highest score with their position and distance to the first; and the Scoring Trend names each operator and its latest score at the end of its line, on the page and in PowerPoint and Word.
- **Titles on one line in the reports:** a slide title too long for one line in the Scoring & GAP Analysis, Network Insights, Non-Qualified Calls and Bundle Report Template exports (PowerPoint and Word) is made smaller, measured with the font the documents are rendered with, instead of taking a second line over the subtitle and the chart or table under it.
- **Vodafone-Three covers and numbered transitions:** the PowerPoint template is `assets/powerpoint-templates/Template_VodafoneThree.pptx` (formerly `Template_01.pptx`): the covers and transitions of every report (PowerPoint, Word and the PPT Dashboards viewer) have the Vodafone-Three red with the Ericsson mark in outline behind them and the Vodafone-Three logo beside the Ericsson logo in white at the top right (slides with a white background keep the dark logo). Every cover has one title and subtitle size and every transition a smaller one, the subtitle right below the room of two title lines, and each transition shows its counter above its title, 01, 02… and 2.1, 2.1.1… below them: a Bundle Report Template numbers its sections and, below them, each scoring and environment, each Dashboard and its PPT Template transitions (also as the headings of its Word pages); a Scoring report with several scenarios opens each one with its own transition. The cover of a Dashboard lists its Campaigns, Scope, Regions and Cities in white, and the viewer uses the light titles of the template.
- **Stored Network Insights analyses:** the newest 500 analyses are kept (not only the latest 40), and the Summary Network Insights exports and Bundle Report Templates use and keep them too, so the same selection is not analysed again; the Network Deployment tables of the exports name only the date of the inventory snapshot of each network.
- **Favourites Filters and Scoring report templates on their own:** the Favourites Filters of Scoring & GAP Analysis travel as the **Scoring & GAP Favourites Filters** element of Import / Export / Transfer, with Full Workspace transfers and with backups, without their results (each finds its CDRs by file, or, with a Campaign choice such as Latest campaign, takes those it stands for in the receiving workspace) and with their Geographical Areas & Scoring Trends (Main Cities kept as such) and Focus operators, and the saved configurations of the Scoring report editor as the **Scoring & GAP Report Templates** element (importing it replaces those with the same name); the Scoring & GAP Analysis Methodologies no longer carry either, and its packages and backups made before still restore them.
- **Network Insights exports:** the NR quality per carrier table shows its RSRP and SINR classes as coloured bars in PowerPoint and Word, the coverage and interference maps take the room of area tables without rows, the total bandwidth ladders of many groups wrap onto more rows so they show larger, and what the ladders show is a note at the foot of their slide (in the colour of notes) instead of lines under its title.
- **Module exports composed as the bundle reports:** the PowerPoint and Word exports of Scoring & GAP Analysis, Network Insights and Non-Qualified Calls (on their page and as Reporting Jobs artifacts) are composed from the same slides as their section of a Bundle Report Template, so a change shows in both: the cover of the module, joined and readable tables, small components sharing a slide and the closing slide, and in Word the cover on a page of its own and portrait pages with as many components as fit. Word documents and previews render in seconds instead of about a minute per document, as the slide layouts no slide uses are left out of the rendering. A table too small to read continues on more slides only when they make its values larger: a wide table of few rows (such as Under 25 MHz by location) keeps one slide instead of one row per slide.
- **Scoring slides of the operators:** Position per environment, Biggest levers, GAP by part and Points Lost per Vendor and per kind of site show up to six compared operators per slide (never one alone on the last), the slides of a single campaign show the latest campaign only, the rule of each operator spans all its campaigns in PowerPoint, the names at the end of the Scoring Trend lines no longer overlap, Campaign Comparison shows each operator in its colour and the GAP Analysis tables take the height of the slide down to their color scale (now on one line) with the largest text their rows fit, the KPIs wider when the GAP columns are few.
- **Operators of the Scoring sections:** the Summary (where each operator stands, position and levers) enables every operator by default, the reference included, and Export Selected Scoring Report shows in each section the operators enabled on the page.
- **Workspace Content by module in Export / Transfer and backups:** Current Workspace Content of Export / Transfer and Workspace Content of the backups list their elements under the module where each is managed (first Workspace Config for the Config pages and the Workspace module, then App Logs and the main modules in the order of the menu, with their titles of Interface Settings), the elements of each module alphabetically; the backups list the Workspace Database, Input and Output before them.
- **Favourites Filters:** the Starred Filters of Scoring & GAP Analysis (the Saved Filters marked with the star) are called Favourites Filters everywhere: their panel, the Scoring tab menu and sections bar, the Saved Filters lists, App Events (`saved filter ID 57 (favourite)`) and Help.
- **Overwrite, rename and delete favourite Bundle Report Templates:** **★ Save as favourite** with the name of an existing favourite overwrites it once the user confirms it; **Rename** renames the chosen favourite and the Reporting Jobs based on it that have its name; **Delete** lists the Reporting Jobs that generate the favourite, disables those based on it and leaves every one saying in Artifacts that the associated Bundle Report Template no longer exists (all of it recorded in App Events).
- **One Generate for the Bundle Report Templates:** **Generate** replaces the PowerPoint and Word buttons: it creates the report in both formats with their previews, and the preview chooses the format to check and download (PowerPoint slide by slide, Word page by page in portrait; each report generated opens in PowerPoint), switching between them at once on the same section; its dialog shows the processing time, **Cancel** stops the report and **Hide** lets it go on in the background while the application is used, with a dialog on any page when it is ready (back in Reporting, **Generate** shows its progress again at once and opens the dialog with **Cancel** and **Hide**); **Open last report** opens the last report each user generated in the panel whenever there is one, kept in the workspace with its documents also after a restart until a newer one replaces it; a Word page no longer repeats the title of components with the same title and context.
- **Faster Word versions and previews:** long PowerPoints are converted by several LibreOffice runs at once (one per processor up to four, `DRIVETEST_ANALYZER_LIBREOFFICE_WORKERS`), the covers and the components of the Word versions are rendered at the same time, the PowerPoint preview of a Bundle Report Template is rendered while its Word pages are packed, and the time a conversion may take grows with its slides instead of giving up after five minutes and converting everything again.
- **Reporting Jobs from a favourite Bundle Report Template:** the Job Editor starts from **A Bundle Report Template favourite** (the default for a new job when the workspace has one: the job generates the favourite chosen, in PowerPoint and/or Word, and takes its name) or from **Design the artifacts** (the editor as before); a job that only generates one favourite opens as such. The jobs table lists under the Bundle Report Template artifact each favourite a job generates with what it includes (its sections with their sub-modules, the Dashboards by name) and tells in **Based On** whether the job generates a favourite (**Bundle Report Template**) or artifacts chosen in the editor (**Manual Artifacts**), with badges counting the jobs of each kind and in all beside **Collapse**; editors order the jobs by dragging them (a copy follows its job); exports, transfers and backups name the favourites of each job, so the destination workspace uses its favourites of the same name, and carry the order of the jobs. Every job has one **Theme** (in violet), read by every artifact that supports themes (CDR Analysis, Network Insights, Scoring & GAP Analysis, PPT Dashboards and Bundle Report Templates) and prevailing over the theme of a favourite Bundle Report Template it generates; without favourites, a tip in the Job Editor recalls that designing a Bundle Report Template and saving it as a favourite is the easiest way to create a job; Run History shows the **Size** of all the artifacts of each run.
- **Themes and visual charts in Network Insights:** a **Theme** selector at the start of the floating section bar colours every chart and table of the page and of the PowerPoint and Word exports with the themes of Scoring & GAP Analysis (NetCheck, Ericsson, Vodafone, Midnight, Paper and Heatmap): table headers and rows, quality classes, bands, total bandwidth ladders, the Key Findings and the font and colours of the CDFs and maps (dark result panels on the page in Midnight). The P10, P50 and P90 under each CDF are bands on one scale instead of tables, the low-coverage and high-interference shares of RF Quality have bars and the observed spectrum is a bar of the bands of each group and technology (also on a slide before its table), whose table opens below the bars. Each user keeps one theme per workspace shared with the other themed modules (registered in App Events), and a Bundle Report Template and a Reporting Job give their Network Insights section or artifacts their one theme.
- **Faster Reporting:** the favourite Bundle Report Templates show as soon as the page opens, and the options of the Bundle Report Template and of the Job Editor (and the automatic CDR choices of every artifact) no longer count the rows of every CDR each time: a CDR is counted once per processing.
- **Network Insights panels in categories:** the Analysis Selection panel is called **Launch Analysis**, and the other panels are grouped in three category panels, **Summary** (Overview and Key Findings), **Radio Performance** (Coverage & Interference Maps, RF Quality, Latency & Interactivity, Serving Cells and 5G Availability) and **Network & Spectrum** (Network Snapshot, Network Deployment, Cluster Sites Density and Spectrum), whose entries in the floating section bar and the sections menu open the list of their panels.
- **Modules & Sections:** the Modules and Sections tabs at the left edge become one **Modules & Sections** tab, where Sections was, that opens the whole tree of the modules (Main Modules, Administrative Modules and Documentation), each with its sections and their parts as the lists of the tabs, the module of the page open and marked in green; in a Help chapter it also lists the headings of the document. The documentation pages no longer have their Navigation and Releases tabs at the right edge.
- **Favourites in the starter content:** new workspaces start with the favourite Bundle Report Templates of the UK workspace (Complete Report, Scoring Report and Weekly Dashboards Report in NSA and SA, with the shipped Dashboards of their NR Mode; `assets/bundle-report-template-favourites`) and its four Favourites Filters (Latest Campaign, Previous Campaign, Latest 2 Campaigns and All Campaigns; `assets/scoring-favourites-filters`), which take the CDRs of their Campaign choice once the workspace has them.
- **Faster Saved Filters:** the National & Geographical Areas Trends and the Points Lost Map of each methodology are calculated once and reused by every Saved Filter with the same CDRs, methodology and options (Saved Filters with the same Scoring Trends, *Latest 2 campaigns* with the maps of *Latest campaign* and *Previous campaign*, or the same Saved Filter calculated again), and a map drawn before is added as soon as the scores are saved; with a methodology per campaign, each methodology reads only the CDRs of its campaigns; a Points Lost Map being drawn gives way to the calculation of another Saved Filter queued meanwhile and goes on afterwards; and while nobody uses the application, the Favourites Filters out of date (such as *Latest campaign* once a new campaign is processed) are calculated one after another with their maps, so they open at once.

#### 🐛 Bug fixes:
- **Scoring Methodology selector:** wide enough to show *By campaign (each campaign with its methodology)* whole.
- **Renaming an Operator or Vendor label in the Report Templates:** only their titles, legends and the values of their Operator and Vendor filters follow the new name (with a number such as 3, only the filters and legends), never their layouts or other conditions, so renaming 3 to Three no longer breaks a template's *3 columns* grid; templates it changed before (*Three columns*) work again.
- **GAP and score bars in PowerPoint:** the bars inside the cells of the Scoring and GAP tables keep their side and length on screen in PowerPoint (a GAP behind the reference to the left of the middle, ahead to the right), as in its PDF export and in the other viewers.
- **GAP order of one operator over several campaigns:** the GAP Analysis of a single operator (on the page and its PowerPoint and Word slides with the Priority arrow) orders the KPIs by their GAP in the latest campaign, the other campaigns breaking ties and the KPIs without a GAP in it last, and says so, instead of by the mean of every campaign.
- **Favourite Bundle Report Templates in another workspace:** imported favourites find their Dashboards by name and NR Mode (Dashboards with the same name in NSA and SA are no longer mixed up), so the same Dashboards stay checked and in the same order, and transfers with several elements import the favourites and Reporting Jobs after the Dashboards.
- **Report previews in Docker:** the image installs LibreOffice Writer, so the Word previews are shown, and the previews no longer show the letters of the fonts the image does not have spaced and overlapping (an installed font stands in for them).
- **Reporting keeps its position when reloaded:** reloading the Reporting page, also with the Job Editor reopened with its unsaved changes, keeps its scroll position while the Bundle Report Template, the jobs and their runs fill in.
- **KPI names in the Scoring tables:** in Points lost per KPI and the KPI GAP Profile, a long KPI name wraps beside its R or D badge with its threshold and unit together (*> 5Mbit/s [%]*), never a unit alone on the second line.
- **Operator order in the Scoring results:** every Operator selector and list of operators of the results (Where to act, Connecting Roads, Campaign Comparison…) follows the order of the Operator Maps.
- **Performance owner cards of older Saved Filters:** a card whose Saved Filter was calculated before its points were kept per City or route asks to recalculate it instead of showing 0.0 for Cities, Towns, Roads and railways; Vendor A and Vendor B swap their colours, the same on every view and slide.
- **Legend of the KPI score bands:** the legend of the Scoring tables matches their cells in every theme, on the page and in PowerPoint and Word: the Ericsson and Vodafone cells take the colours of the legend, and the other themes draw the legend as their cells (NetCheck and Umlaut data bars, Heatmap tints, Paper underlined text, Midnight glowing bars).
- **Scoring sections of reports after a recalculation:** a Bundle Report Template or a Reporting Job no longer reuses a Scoring & GAP Analysis section generated before a Saved Filter was calculated again: it generates it again by itself, so a scheduled job sends the current results.
- **Date filter of App Events:** it always lists every date of the events, whatever the other filters.
- **Campaign Comparison in the Scoring documents:** it follows the Scoring Tables in PowerPoint and Word, as on the page, instead of closing the scoring.
- **Calculation panel of Scoring & GAP Analysis:** Campaign and Focus operators are as tall as the other choices of their row.

#### 📚 Documentation:

## Release: v0.7.3
### Release Date: 2026-10-17
#### ⚠️ Breaking Changes:

#### 🌟 New Features:
- **Bandwidth and NR duplex sites in Network Insights:** an **Environments** filter and Split by (big cities, driven and walked, are Cities; medium and small cities, Towns; connecting roads, highways and train routes, Roads and railways, from the G_Level fields of the CDRs) and three Spectrum views. **NR sites by duplex mode** counts the in-service sites with NR TDD, NR FDD, both or only one of them per network of the inventory snapshot, as logical sites (site IDs) and physical sites (sites of any network within 50 m count once, with an All networks row), each kind of site in its own group of columns (the physical ones headed by a red bar), after the Operator, Operator_Vendor, Vendor, Region, Cluster and City filters and split by Region, Cluster, City, RAN Vendor or Host Network. **Bandwidth per band** counts the samples of each serving band at each DL bandwidth of its primary carrier (a bandwidth under 3% of every band goes to an **Other** column), and **Bandwidth of each test: LTE + NR** draws, per group and environment, the share of tests in each class from under 25 to 200+ MHz (the aggregated LTE bandwidth plus the NR PCell bandwidth) as a stepped chart with that title, the share under 25 MHz beside each ladder (in the red of its block) and the share at 100 MHz or more under it (in bold for the largest of each environment), explained in a red line under the title that also names the group with the largest share under 25 MHz in each environment (also in the summaries), for the download tests or every CDR Data test; **Ladders per** splits its groups by the kind of serving site or the RAN Vendor of the serving cell, and **Download tests by location** gives per City the share of the download tests under 25 MHz and failed or at 5 Mbit/s or less. The PowerPoint and Word summaries include them, and Reporting Jobs can split their Network Insights artifact by Environment.
- **Latency & Interactivity and NR quality per carrier in Network Insights:** a **Latency & Interactivity** panel ranks the groups of each environment by the failures, round trip (median and P90), packet loss and jitter of their gaming and video chat tests, and RF Quality adds the median SS-RSRP and SS-SINR and quality classes of each NR band, also in PowerPoint and Word.
- **Failures by Type, Technology & Serving Cell in Non-Qualified Calls:** a panel with the result of the calls per environment as a share of every call, the technology at the failure against the NetCheck classification, the calls and NQ rate per Vendor and host network of the serving cell and the sites with the most Non-Qualified Calls, drilling down into the calls and included in PowerPoint, Word and Reporting (the CDRs are indexed again in the background to add the serving cell).

#### 🚀 Enhancements:
- **Cascading filters of every CDR of the NR Mode:** the CDR Filter Facets also keep the test environment of each CDR (read again once, in the background, for the CDRs read before), so the Environments filter of Network Insights lists only the environments the other filters leave and narrows their lists in turn.
- **Area, operators and vendors in the Network Insights charts and tables:** as in Scoring & GAP Analysis, the title of every chart and table of Network Insights ends with pills naming its area (the City, Cluster or Region filters, else **National**), its operators (**All Operators** without an Operator filter), its vendors (**All Vendors** without a Vendor filter) and the Environments when chosen: the filters of the analysis shown for the measurements, those of their own selection for the NR sites by duplex mode and the site tables, and none for the panels of the whole inventory. A chart or table without a title has them in a line above it, its larger view shows them, and the PowerPoint and Word summaries name them under the title of every slide and section.
- **Export choices for a Saved Filter:** Export Selected Scoring Report (PPT/Word) asks with checkboxes for the scorings (Best Network and/or Most Reliable) and the environments (All Environments and/or each environment), Best Network and All Environments ticked by default, and the export log records the choice.
- **Compact Word reports of Scoring & GAP Analysis:** the Word documents of Export Selected Scoring Report, Generate Scoring Report and the Reporting Jobs put the slides of each section on shared portrait pages without their empty bands, every section from a new page, merge consecutive covers into one and give the cover a whole page with its background.

#### 🐛 Bug fixes:
- **Section bar menus on phones:** the list of parts that opens from the section bar of every module is as tall as its parts and scrolls within the screen, instead of stretching to the bottom of the screen with wide gaps between its entries on phones.

#### 📚 Documentation:

## Release: v0.7.2
### Release Date: 2026-10-16
#### ⚠️ Breaking Changes:

#### 🌟 New Features:
- **CDR Campaign:** the Final, Weekly and Daily CDR tables of Workspace show the **Campaign** of each CDR after its name, as `2026-Q2` (NSA or SA is its NR Mode), proposed from the quarter and the year (or the dates) of its file name (blank when the name has neither), each campaign in its own colour, editable in place (any campaign, with those of the other CDRs as suggestions), recorded in App Events and carried with the datasets in packages, transfers and backups. The header of the Datasets panel counts the campaigns, CDRs, Network Inventories and Geographic Datasets of the workspace and shows the size of all its datasets (MB, or GB from 1 GB); each CDR card shows its number of campaigns and of CDRs and their size beside its icon and its table is grouped by campaign (newest first, the CDRs without one last, each group with its number of CDRs and their MB) and open sorted by Campaign, NR Mode and CDR Type.
- **CDRs chosen from the NR Mode and Campaign:** PPT Dashboards, Scoring & GAP Analysis, Network Insights and Non-Qualified Calls have no CDR selector: they use the Data, Voice and Speech CDRs of their NR Mode and Campaign filter that the combined CDR tables include (the Final CDR of a campaign before its Weekly and Daily CDRs, a Weekly CDR before its Daily ones, or the In Combined? choice of Workspace), listed read-only as **CDRs used** by type, with their stage and campaigns, following every change of the filters at once. The Campaign filter of Dashboards, Scoring, Network Insights and Reporting Jobs (also the Non-Qualified Calls artifact) also offers first **Latest campaign**, **Previous campaign** (the one before it), **Latest 2 campaigns** and **All available campaigns**, made again whenever they are used so the campaigns that arrive later are included (in Scoring, the campaigns with Data, Voice and Speech CDRs), each one standing alone; a Scoring Saved Filter keeps the choice and follows it whenever it is opened; a new Dashboard follows its newest two campaigns (the newest one in Multivendor Operator_Vendor), and Scoring opens on **Latest campaign** (the newest campaign with Data, Voice and Speech CDRs), naming and leaving out the campaigns without every type. Non-Qualified Calls has **NR Mode** as its first General Filter instead of CDRs, which takes the campaigns of the other NR Mode out of Campaign (the CDR filter stays with the optional CDR column) and shows each call with the version of the CDR the combined tables include. The Export PPT dialog of Dashboards and the Dashboard, Scoring and Network Insights artifacts of Reporting Jobs choose **Campaigns** instead of CDRs (in Reporting, one list right after NR Mode) and list the CDRs they use now, the Scoring artifact scores each campaign with its methodology by default, and the Non-Qualified Calls artifact offers NR Mode and then Campaign with the campaigns of the NR Modes chosen; the CDR Analysis artifact chooses its CDRs from its NR Mode and Campaigns too, while the CDR Analysis module keeps choosing its CDR. Dashboards, Scoring selections and Reporting Jobs saved before take the campaigns of their CDRs (their newest CDRs become Latest 2 campaigns in Dashboards and Latest campaign in Reporting Jobs).
- **NetCheck 2025 methodology:** a new Scoring methodology for the campaigns up to 2026-Q1, from the NET CHECK 2025/26 Benchmarking Methodology: the KPIs, thresholds and Data points of NetCheck 2026 with **CALL SETUP TIME > 15 s [%]** instead of > 10 s and the 2025/26 voice points (classic calls 252, WhatsApp calls 84). The shipped methodologies apply to their campaigns (NetCheck 2025 up to 2026-Q1, NetCheck 2026 for 2026-Q2, NetCheck 2026-Q3 from 2026-Q3), so **By campaign** scores each campaign with its own; NetCheck 2026 stays the active methodology of a new workspace.

#### 🚀 Enhancements:
- **CDR Type and CDR Stage columns:** in the CDR tables of Workspace the Input Type column is called **CDR Type**, follows the NR Mode and shows Data, Voice and Speech in a selector of a different colour each that changes a CDR to another type (after a confirmation, reprocessing it from its source file and moving it to the combined table of that type) (Data in orange and Voice in raspberry, apart from the colours of In Combined? and SA), and the Final, Weekly and Daily column (and its upload choice) is called **CDR Stage**, follows the CDR Type and shows Final in teal so it is not confused with SA.
- **Points Lost Map progress:** the background task card of a Points Lost Map shows a real progress bar, the step it is on (the CDR being read, the CDR type and group of tests being mapped and, by campaign, the methodology) and about how long it has left.
- **Saved Filters order:** the Saved Filters lists show only the starred Saved Filters, in a fixed order that opening one no longer changes: a newly starred one goes first and dragging a card by the tab on its left in the **Starred Filters** panel moves it, with a horizontal line where it will go (recorded in App Events and kept in exports and transfers); the opened unstarred one shows in the selected card of the Scoring Results dropdown. The name of a Saved Filter is a badge on top of its card, and **✎** names it in a dialog of the application that keeps its filters in sight above the name field.
- **Scoring & GAP Results and Starred Filters panels:** the results panel of Scoring & GAP Analysis is headed **Calculated Scoring & GAP Results**, with **Scoring & GAP Results** as its title, and the Saved Filters panel is called **Starred Filters**; the Scoring tab menu and the sections bar list them with these names and open them.
- **Cascading filters of every CDR of the NR Mode:** the filters of PPT Dashboards, Scoring & GAP Analysis and Network Insights list the values of every CDR of the NR Mode, with the labels of the Operator, Vendor and Campaign Maps, each one only those the other filters leave (choosing Vodafone as Operator leaves in Vendor only its Vendors); an open list stays open while values are checked, and PPT Dashboards fills them as soon as a Dashboard opens, without waiting for its preparation. They come from the combinations of Campaign, Operator, Operator_Vendor, Vendor, Region, Cluster and City each CDR keeps in the new **CDR Filter Facets** table of Database Management, read once when the CDR is processed (in the background for older CDRs), so filling them never reads the rows of the CDRs.
- **Dashboard Scope & Filters:** the Dataset Universe is gone: the Scope, the vendor comparison and **Date from** / **Date to**, now the first Default Filters before Campaign, are applied, saved and reloaded with the filters in the renamed **Dashboard Scope & Filters** panel, which lists the CDRs used below the Scope.
- **Default content of the UK workspace:** new workspaces start with the Reporting Jobs *Weekly NetCheck Report (@latest campaign)* (disabled), *All Modules Report (@latest 2 campaigns)* and *Scoring Report (@All Campaigns)*, every Scoring artifact by campaign methodology, and with the Dashboards and Report Templates of the UK workspace (NetCheck CDR Report and NetCheck CDR Report (Main Cities) in NSA and SA, RF Quality (RSRP & SINR), Tableau Dashboards and Tableau Charts), the templates without Operator filters (nor Vendor filters by operator); existing workspaces keep their own content.
- **Edit Saved Filters:** the **✎** of a Saved Filter keeps **Save Name** and adds **Edit Filters**, which opens its selection in a dialog with the Calculation panel to change its NR Mode, Campaign, filters, Split by, GAP reference, National & area summary or methodology, with its CDRs chosen from the NR Mode and Campaign; **Save Changes** calculates it again keeping its name, star and place. Opening a Saved Filter lists only its CDRs with its campaigns.
- **Saved Filter cards:** the date and filters of a Saved Filter card are in a blue box with its status, and its Split by, GAP Reference, National & area summary and Scoring Methodology values are pills, each in its own colour (**By campaign** alone, followed by the methodology of each campaign), on detail lines of the same size.
- **Dropdowns below their field:** every dropdown of the application opens its own list below the field (above it when there is no room below, also in dialogs and over panels) instead of the browser's, which macOS opens over the field, with a search box for long lists. The lists are as wide as their values, each on one line (opening towards the left near the edge of the screen), with their values closer together, and the Campaign lists, with their automatic choices first, are taller. On small screens the menus of the main menu are compact and never taller than most of the screen.
- **Tabs always in sight:** the tabs of the modules stay at the top of the window while a page scrolls (unless they would take more than a third of a small screen), with the sections bar and the other bars that stay at the top below them.
- **Area, operators and vendors in the Scoring charts and tables:** the title of every chart and table of Scoring & GAP Analysis (Scoring Charts, Scoring Tables, GAP Analysis, KPI GAP Profile, Points Lost Map and Campaign Comparison) ends with pills naming the area it shows (the City, Cluster or Region of its comparison context or location, else the City, Cluster or Region filters of the Saved Filter, else **National**), its operators (**All Operators** without an Operator filter) and its vendors (**All Vendors** without a Vendor filter), two names and how many more when there are over three, also in its larger view; the PowerPoint and Word reports name them at the end of the subtitle of each chart and table slide, so a copied chart or table still tells them.

#### 🐛 Bug fixes:
- **Scoring & GAP Analysis on phones:** the Scoring Methodology pill, the comparison context and Scoring Tables lists and the GAP Analysis tables fit the screen (long options are shortened and wide tables scroll inside their box) instead of running past it.
- **Saved Filters calculating again:** opening a Saved Filter whose CDRs were reprocessed follows its new calculation to the end and shows its result when it completes, instead of staying at 2% (or showing its old result) until another Saved Filter was opened, whether the list of Saved Filters arrives before or after its answer. A Saved Filter opened from the Saved Filters panel takes the page to its Scoring Results.
- **Use newer CDRs:** the button of a Saved Filter replaces its CDRs with the newer ones in the same Saved Filter (keeping its name, star and place) and calculates it again, instead of creating another Saved Filter; a Saved Filter that already had that selection is merged into it, and App Events record the CDRs replaced. In the Starred Filters panel each card has a checkbox on top of the tab on its left, and **Select all** and **Use newer CDRs** on top of the panel update every checked Saved Filter with newer CDRs at once, calculating them in the background while the open one stays.
- **Dashboard Save Filters:** Save Filters saves and applies the Scope, dates and filters of the Dashboard, so nothing is left unapplied or unsaved and leaving the page no longer warns about unsaved changes.
- **Main Cities in Dashboards:** choosing Main Cities in the City filter of a Dashboard, its Export PPT dialog or a Reporting Dashboard entry saves the Main Cities choice instead of the cities of that moment, so the Dashboard follows the Main Cities of the workspace; Dashboards saved before with exactly the current Main Cities take it once.
- **Closing a Dashboard while it opens:** closing a Dashboard (or opening another one) while it is still checking its prepared data, for example right after importing it, no longer shows the error "Cannot read properties of null (reading 'hidden_filters')".

#### 📚 Documentation:
