- `pyproject.toml` now reports the application version and lists every directly imported runtime dependency (`httpx`, `pillow` and `certifi` were missing or only listed for development); `requirements.txt` pins Pillow and certifi explicitly. The release helper also updates the `pyproject.toml` version, and the unused `tools/BuildBinary.py` script copied from another project was removed.
- Dashboard Universe selectors, the PPT export universe dialog (whose title shows the NR Mode, for example `CDR datasets (NSA)`), default universes (latest two CDRs per type, or one for Multivendor) and pre-cached universes only consider CDRs of the Dashboard's NR Mode. Changing a Dashboard's NR Mode selects the newest CDRs of the new mode.
- Dashboard pre-caching covers every CDR plus the newest one, two, three and four CDRs of each type, skipping duplicate or impossible universes. It runs on its own low-priority worker instead of the shared background scheduler: open Dashboards are pre-cached first, closed ones pause while an open Dashboard needs work, and foreground preparations and PPT exports pause it until they finish. PPT exports are no longer blocked by pre-caching; the export job pre-caches its chosen universe before applying its own selections. Manage Dashboards shows `Checking Cache` and `Pre-Caching Universe n/m` (with `· Waiting` while queued or paused).
- The first action of every E2E Dashboard filter selector is **Select All / None** (formerly **All values**) and toggles between selecting every value and none; selecting all still keeps an open selection that includes values from future CDRs.
- A Dashboard that opens with a Title Slide shows the Scope, the selected Regions and the Cities directly below the cover subtitle and its decorative line, with the same layout in the viewer and in the exported PPT and in distinct green, cyan and pink accents. The PPT cover subtitle now keeps the template text instead of repeating the Scope, and Title and Transition subtitles in exported PPTs (Dashboards and E2E Reporting) use the template's yellow accent.
- The Dashboard viewer has a **Scope** selector before Dashboard Filters. Changing it asks for confirmation, because every chart is rendered again, and then applies the new scope with its default Dataset Universe.
- The Report Template Editor has **Find & Replace** (also Ctrl/Cmd+F) to search every template cell, move between matches and replace in the current cell or the whole template; replacements remain unsaved edits until the template is saved.
- Dashboard Datasets & Filters is now a sub-panel of Manage Dashboards: **Open Filters** shows it directly below the corresponding Dashboard row and **Close Filters** removes it, instead of a separate panel below the library.
- Dashboard PowerPoint file and folder names include the NR Mode (`NSA` or `SA`) before the Dashboard name, for example `20260927_101500 - NSA - Dashboard - Operator Comparison - All Regions.pptx`.
- Dashboard names are unique per NR Mode, so an NSA and an SA Dashboard can share a name; copies and imports only add a suffix when the name is taken in the same NR Mode.
- The Dashboards and Report Templates tables are sorted by NR Mode (NSA first) and then name, ignoring an NSA/SA marker written in the name. The Report Templates table calls its type column **NR Mode** and adds **Last Updated by**, the user behind the latest import, edit, rename, duplicate, default change or NR Mode move (stored in `report_templates.updated_by`).
- Dashboard PowerPoint export requests return as soon as the job is created. Resolving the export universe, cover labels, Filters column and file name now happens inside the background job, so a busy server no longer delays queuing; problems such as a universe without valid CDRs mark the job as failed with the reason.
- The Dashboard viewer shows the NR Mode before the Dashboard name, a larger violet slide title and golden-yellow subtitle, and the applied Scope, Regions and Cities in distinct colours separated by bold dots. Its lavender Scope selector matches the adjacent action buttons in height and text size.

#### 🐛 Bug fixes:
- Multivendor E2E Reporting chart-set jobs no longer fail on their first chart with an undefined `prepare_multivendor_catalog_entry` error.
- Dashboards whose Report Template cannot be read now return the template validation message instead of a server error.
- Query Builder accepts selected CDRs whose names contain quotes or apostrophes; `source_dataset_name` is now emitted as a correctly escaped SQL literal.
- Hierarchical failure-count charts draw nested row separators from their parent label column on every row, instead of shifting them to the left after the first bar with a count label.
- E2E Reporting chart-set identifiers use the configured Application Config timezone, so they match the displayed generation time.
- The Dashboard PPT job Filters column keeps the requested date range (for example `Oldest to Newest`) when an already prepared Dashboard snapshot is reused for the export.
- Sign-in rejects a malformed stored password hash instead of raising a server error, and dataset workers no longer fail when their CDR is deleted while it is still processing.
- A Dashboard marked Ready no longer returns to preparation after another Dashboard is opened: adding or filling combined-table columns keeps prepared universes valid, and only row changes invalidate them.
- Reduced Dashboard timeouts (504) and memory pressure on smaller servers: Dashboard preparation runs in the background while the browser polls its progress, read-only requests retry transient gateway errors, status checks reuse their results and read universe manifests directly, identical cached universes share one snapshot, and only recently used snapshots keep chart frames in memory (`DASHBOARD_ANALYTIC_DASHBOARD_FRAME_CACHE_SNAPSHOTS`). A new preparation now only supersedes the same user's earlier request.
- The Materialization status bar shows 100% when all fields are up to date instead of 0%.
- PowerPoint Job actions such as Relaunch no longer ignore clicks: the jobs table is only rebuilt when its data changes and never while a mouse button is held over it, and Relaunch gives immediate busy feedback and ignores repeated clicks until the server answers.
- The Dashboard library **Generate Dashboard PPT** action (renamed from Generate PPT Dashboard) is available immediately after the table is redrawn instead of staying disabled until the next status poll, following the same rule as the status sync: only Dashboards without usable CDRs cannot be exported.
- The PPT export dialog explains why **Generate PPT** is waiting (`Loading values…` while the Operators, Vendors, Regions and Cities load, or a hint when no CDR is selected), and confirming shows a `Queuing Dashboard PPT` progress overlay until the server has queued the job.
- Dashboard covers (viewer and PPT) keep their Region and City lines when the per-CDR catalogue lacks those values or cannot be loaded: the Dashboard's own Region and City filter values, which resolve source-column aliases, complete them. A PPT job opened with View Dashboard Snapshot shows exactly the Scope, Regions and Cities of its exported cover; jobs exported earlier rebuild them from their Filters column. A selection that covers every Region or City, from either the CDR catalogue or the Dashboard's filter options and ignoring case and spaces, is labelled `All Regions` / `All Cities` instead of listing the individual names.
- Title and Transition slide subtitles in the Dashboard viewer use the template's yellow accent instead of a grey colour inherited from the viewer panel, and structural slide text scales with the slide so it no longer overflows short viewers. The viewer actions no longer overlap the slide selector on narrower screens.
- The automated test suite passes again: tests wait for isolated dataset workers and combined CDR rebuilds, and reflect the E2E Reporting access rules, renamed mapping controls and current chart rendering contracts.

#### 📚 Documentation:
- README and Deployment Configuration list the `demo` bootstrap account with its current `user-viewer` role. Workspace Config documents the ascending order of explicit distribution ranges, and Project Structure describes how the test client waits for background dataset processing, the release helper and `pyproject.toml`. Workspace Management documents NR Mode, and E2E Dashboards and Technical Considerations describe NR-aware universes, background pre-caching priorities, status labels and the frame-cache setting.

---

## Release: v0.4.0
### Release Date: 2026-09-25
#### ⚠️ Breaking Changes:
- The Config tab opens Application Config at `/application-config` or Workspace Config at `/workspace-config`. Workspace Config contains Report Templates Management, Operator Mappings and Vendor Mappings at dedicated `/workspace-config/` routes; previous `/config`, `/app-config` and `/admin/` routes for these controls are removed. Both pages are available to `user-editor`, `admin` and `super-admin`; the former `user` role is now `user-viewer` and cannot edit either page. Help has separate Application Config, Workspace Config, Administrator Config and App Logs chapters under Administrative Modules.

#### 🌟 New Features:
- Added workspace-scoped Geospatial Region Mappings. GeoJSON and zipped shapefiles can be selected during CDR import (the newest mapping is preselected), populate blank `Region` values from Data, Voice or Speech coordinates, and can be assigned or cleared per CDR or in bulk from the Dataset queue.
- Added a workspace-scoped **Query Builder** module for safe read-only analysis over one or more selected individual Data, Voice and Speech CDRs. It exposes temporary `selected_data`, `selected_voice` and `selected_speech` views with original CDR fields and source identifiers, supports saved reusable queries and CSV export.

#### 🚀 Enhancements:
- Dashboard PowerPoint exports from the Dashboard library request Operators, Vendors, Regions and Cities in the Dataset Universe panel whenever multiple values are available, including for Multivendor Comparison. Their searchable selectors appear in that order, default to every value, and no longer block Generate PPT after a valid CDR and date selection. Explicit dialog selections control the generated PPT and job Filters column even when the Dashboard has different saved filters. The dialog remembers Scope, CDRs, dates and selector choices per user and Dashboard in the browser, and preserves partial choices when the available values refresh. Vendor, Region and City choices come from a persisted per-CDR catalogue instead of scanning combined CDR rows; existing CDRs without a catalogue are backfilled once from their individual materialized tables. Their subpanels remain visible with a clear disabled explanation when the selected CDRs have zero or one value. Workspace Config provides a transferable Main Cities list with two-column selection controls; its `Main Cities` shortcut is available in both Dashboard Default Filters and PPT export. The catalogue is refreshed when a CDR is processed, reprocessed, mapped or cleared and is visible in Manage Database. Multivendor Comparison is disabled in both the Dashboard Universe and PPT export Scope selectors unless the workspace has at least two distinct cached Vendors; server validation applies the same requirement to selected CDRs. The first PPT slide uses the report Scope as its subtitle and shows selected Regions and Cities in aligned accent colours. Generated PPT filenames and work folders use a `yyyymmdd_HHMMSS` timestamp, Dashboard name, Scope and selected Regions in that order; partial Regions are joined with ` + ` and complete selections show `All Regions`. Colliding timestamps advance to the next free second, and long names fit filesystem component limits. Operators, Vendors and Cities remain in the job Filters column, which uses `All Operators`, `All Vendors`, `All Regions` or `All Cities` when the full selection is included.
- Opening E2E Dashboards now queues the reusable data warm-up for every Dashboard in the library immediately, without requiring the filters panel or Dashboard viewer to be opened first; library status advances from Checking to Preparing or Ready as the background work progresses.
- After five minutes without an interactive application request, Dashboard cache warm-up also starts from any module when both background schedulers are idle. Passive status polling does not reset this quiet period, and each idle interval is warmed at most once.
- Dataset bulk operations now submit their complete batch atomically before dispatch, so Map Vendor & Region follows the displayed priority instead of racing the browser submission order. Region mapping assets precede Vendor mapping assets, while CDRs inside the same bulk batch run in descending numeric ID order; individually enqueued CDRs preserve FIFO order. Restart recovery restores those same saved batch and FIFO priorities, and the floating task card mirrors the effective queue rules. The Map CDR fields dialog is wider and now presents a concise green Region mapping rule alongside the Vendor mapping explanation.
- Export-package jobs now appear in their own green floating `Export tasks` card in the active Workspace task dock, stacked above the yellow Workspace card whenever both queues have work, making the independent export scheduler queue visible separately from Workspace work.
- Server-to-server transfers now start their offer, package stream and remote-progress polling on a dedicated connection thread instead of waiting behind the shared CDR background queue. Incoming offers poll sequentially at a visibility-aware cadence, retry immediately after focus or network recovery, and leave a persistent review reminder plus a System Tasks review action until they are accepted or rejected.
- Query Builder retains Chart Builder's orange palette, separate save/load panels, cancellable runs, saved-query editing and JSON/CSV export. Assistance Mode combines selected CDR types with `UNION ALL`, supports **AND**/**OR** filters, field selection, sorting and row limits, and offers compatible source-name choices for `source_dataset_name` filters using **Is** or **Is not**. **Show all fields** starts unchecked with `source_dataset_name` and `cdr_type` preselected; **All rows** omits SQL `LIMIT`. Results paginate in batches of up to 50 rows, report current-page row and column counts plus the total row count, and provide first, previous, next and last page controls above and below the table. Result-column menus filter distinct values across all pages, and the filtered total drives navigation. **Copy** copies the visible page's headers and rows as TSV; **Export CSV** downloads all rows and columns in the filtered result without a row cap. The SQL preview stays visible with guidance when selections or filters are incomplete, and completed conditions continue to appear. The mode buttons are named **Assistance Mode** and **SQL Mode**; the manual editor is labelled **SQL Query**, and the initial guidance preview allows switching between modes. **Clear Query** confirms before resetting the editor, controls, sources and current results without deleting saved queries. Saved generated SQL restores Assistance Mode controls when it matches the builder; custom or unsupported SQL remains in SQL Mode. Saved queries remain compatible with existing SQL and JSON/backup transfers; the library refreshes after save, supports confirmed deletion, and Database Management labels the table **Saved Queries**.
- Count Stacked Horizontal Bars keep complete column aggregation titles at every hierarchy level in the Dashboard canvas and exported PowerPoint charts, progressively reducing font size at each level and within narrow columns while reserving space between the final header row and the data.
- E2E Reporting is enabled only for super-admins and the EJAITUR user; other users see no E2E Reporting entries in the top navigation or Modules menu and cannot access its page or API routes directly. Their Help navigation omits the E2E Reporting chapter, renumbers later sections, removes links to that chapter and blocks its direct document routes.
- Help Navigation groups documents with unnumbered filenames under General, Main Modules, Administrative Modules and Reference in both the inline panel and navigation drawer. Docker Deployment follows Deployment Configuration in General; App Logs leads the administrative documents, Administrator Config ends them, and Readme and Changelog lead Reference. Workspace Config owns the Report Template Editor and authoring reference, while Administrator Config documents the Operator and Vendor mapping database tables. Access rules still hide restricted chapters, the Web Interface document is named Web Interfaces, and Help Home opens after login and from the authenticated root.
- The top navigation groups Chart Builder and Query Builder under a Builders dropdown with their orange palette. The Modules sidebar lists Application Logs first and Administrator Config last within Administrative Modules, uses more of the viewport height, and keeps Admin as its top tab.
- Each chart in the Dashboard viewer exposes its Chart Definition tab directly, with the same preview and template-update actions as the expanded chart viewer.
- Dashboard Default Filters now place Market, Region and City in the first row, followed by Campaign, Operator and Vendor. Saving or applying a changed Dataset Universe reloads the filter values; choosing All values persists an open selection that includes values from future CDRs, even when the current universe has one value. PowerPoint export pairs Operators with Vendors and Regions with Cities, aligning the controls within each row.

#### 🐛 Bug fixes:
- Workspace configuration exports and server transfers now read Auto-calculated Field definitions directly without triggering CDR materialization. Progress estimates no longer include the application database for workspace-only packages.
- Export packages use a dedicated worker queue that recovers after server restart and can be stopped immediately even if a source read is stalled. Package imports and incoming transfers can be stopped before data replacement begins; an active import rejects Stop.
- Workspace data jobs now run in mapping-first and dataset-ID order, followed by combined CDR recreation and materialization, with one heavy job per Workspace and a server concurrency cap that reserves an interactive CPU.
- Large CDR source files run in isolated, lower-priority workers with bounded native thread use and cross-process Stop. Dataset source processing and restart recovery use isolated server workers detached from the HTTP response; closing the app terminates their child processes, including after abrupt parent exit via a worker watchdog.
- Queued datasets can be stopped individually, released immediately for retry, and show an empty 0% progress track. Stop markers belong to the task's Workspace so workers in inactive Workspaces also receive them; late progress cannot overwrite a completed result, and a previously inconsistent completed profile becomes retryable while the remaining queue waits.
- Automatic materialization now exposes progress, elapsed time, Stop, and App Event lifecycle entries, while opening the Workspace page no longer starts reconciliation. Dataset actions retain their live controls across polling, queued cards describe the current blocking task rather than always citing mappings, and active datasets publish their actual processing step in the card and Workspace table.
- Database restores report completed archive components and files rather than a fixed percentage, and import and restore jobs record lifecycle App Events. The periodic backup scheduler stops with the server, and legacy unbracketed calculated-field rules remain readable during background reconciliation.
- Query Builder no longer recreates or overwrites a hardcoded query when its page opens; its library comes from the active workspace database. Query imports match renamed sources by CDR type and file content, and report missing or ambiguous sources. Read-only execution, filter lookups and CSV exports now see committed SQLite WAL rows. Runs and page changes use background status polling to avoid proxy timeouts, support cancellation and show errors in the results panel. Saving validates SQL without running the full query, and complete result pages avoid repeating the query solely to count rows.
- Collapsed side navigation tabs leave only a subtle edge shadow and reveal through an invisible CSS hover area, which stays responsive while background tasks run. Task cards leave that edge clear, and Help Sections lists the current document headings.
- The `user-editor` role can access Edit Template and Auto-Calculated Fields in both Dashboard viewers, save Chart Definition changes to the Report Template, and delete individual or all generated Dashboard PPT jobs.
- Dashboard filter selectors now expose every distinct value in selected CDRs, including values beyond the former 50 and 200 item profile limits. Existing truncated profiles are completed from source rows and stale filter-selection caches are refreshed.
- Workspace Config panel and table headers use dark teal backgrounds with light text so they remain distinct from the light panel content.
- Report Template saves validate the complete grid before writing, return without rebuilding the editor or waiting for Dashboard comment reconciliation and combined CDR updates, and refresh the Dashboard viewer when the editor closes. Editing reports malformed rows and filters before save. The background CDR update runs when a newly used source field is absent from a combined table; unused columns remain available for later templates. Inline renames send one request when leaving the name field or pressing Enter, keep the request alive during navigation, and preserve special characters in template action URLs.
- Partial Report Template imports, transfers and selective restores now synchronize the destination library without case-only duplicates. Local templates used by saved Dashboards retain their definitions when Dashboards are not included in the package; packages that include Dashboards replace those templates along with the Dashboard definitions.

#### 📚 Documentation:
- Product Overview now includes Query Builder and links to its detailed guide.

---

## Release: v0.3.3
### Release Date: 2026-09-21
#### ⚠️ Breaking Changes:
- Report Template visual configuration headers have been renamed from `Label` to `Label Position` and from `Label Color` to `Label Format`. Existing templates are read through compatibility aliases and gain the current headers when saved, but external template tooling and imports that write either former header must use the new names; `Label Format` is now a JSON list rather than a standalone colour value.

#### 🌟 New Features:
- Report Template KPIs now accept explicit `SUM`, `COUNT`, `COUNTD`, `AVERAGE`/`AVG`/`MEAN`, `MAX`, `MIN` and `MEDIAN` expressions. Cell Assistance, Chart Definition and the E2E Reporting chart viewer can compose these expressions from the selected CDR field and restore the chosen operation when reopened.
- Added the `Dynamic Table` chart type. It pivots Column Aggregation dimensions above the selected measure, preserves separate row-hierarchy fields, suppresses repeated parent labels and fits additional rows for Tableau-style validation tables. A pivot dimension repeated under Rows is treated only as a column, and tables with more than 20 distinct pivot values request a narrowing filter instead of compressing into an unreadable grid.
- Admin Import / Export / Transfer now supports multi-select content packages. Selecting a Full Workspace removes and disables its separately listed workspace elements, while Full Environment becomes exclusive because it already includes Config and every workspace selected in its confirmation dialog.
- Combined exports and transfers are packaged as a single bundle that Import applies in order. Long selected-package descriptions remain contained within the panel and are visually truncated instead of widening the layout.
- Added configurable thematic palettes and explicit ordering for canonical Operators and Vendors. Reporting, Dashboard, Chart Builder and Dataset Analysis charts use the configured colours and order for Operator, Subscriber, Vendor and combined Operator_Vendor dimensions; Vendor colour takes precedence for combined identities, falls back to the Operator colour when no Vendor exists, and is preserved consistently across static, Canvas and CDF renders. CDF Vendor groups retain distinct solid/dashed Operator strokes, campaign recency uses line width rather than colour variants, and horizontal legends adapt their rows to prevent overlap.
- Added the Vendor Mappings manager alongside Operator Mappings, with Move Up/Move Down controls, editable aliases and colour pickers. Changes refresh only the open panel and preserve its scroll position; canonical renames migrate exact Report Template and saved-Dashboard references safely, and mappings remain portable through Import / Export / Transfer, Full Environment and Backup / Restore. Combined identities resolve the longest configured Operator prefix while exact identities such as `VF_SA` remain intact, and the former chart-specific order and colour tables are migrated into the workspace-managed component.
- Added per-chart visual configuration to Report Templates and Chart Definition: `Axis X Range`, `Axis Y Range`, `Label Position`, `Label Format`, `Legend Format`, `Exclude Null/Empty` and `Exclude Zero` now apply to every chart family. Label positions support `None`, `Top`, `Up`, `Middle` and `Down`.
- Label Format and Legend Format share Cell Assistance and store an optional JSON list with hexadecimal colour, font, `Bold`/`Italic` styles and `Small`/`Medium`/`Large` relative sizes. Legend Format styles legend captions and row/column aggregation headings, whose successive levels on each axis decrease by 2 pt independently; empty formatting retains automatic colours and existing defaults, and the settings work consistently in live Dashboards, static/PPT charts and hover geometry.

#### 🚀 Enhancements:
- Consolidated macOS launcher packaging into a single reproducible build script that compiles and signs the universal ARM64/x86_64 application under `macos-launcher/` and writes its installer package under `macos-installer/`, keeping both deliverables out of the project root.
- Canvas percentage charts retain compact one-decimal data labels while their hover tooltips expose two decimals for closer source-data validation.
- Dynamic Tables now start with case-insensitive alphabetical ordering at every row and pivot-column hierarchy level, invalidating older persisted and browser-cached Canvas models that retained source order. Their Canvas headers and hierarchical row members can be dragged with the mouse while retaining the associated values, with child rows and columns constrained to reordering inside their immediate parent group; progressively weighted separators now mark every hierarchy transition on both row and column axes, making first-level groups and each nested subgroup visually distinct.
- Active combined-table recreations now expose a confirmed Stop action directly in their Workspace dataset row, and each running or recoverable Auto-calculated Field task exposes it beside its Materialization status progress bar. Both controls share the cooperative cancellation and live status used by the global background-task panel.
- Materialization cancellation is persisted outside the busy Workspace database and observed between table operations, so it also works when Stop reaches a different web worker.
- CDF charts with more than two campaigns for the same operator now distinguish recency with progressively decreasing line widths across the four most recent campaigns, from the thickest newest curve to the thinnest oldest curve, consistently in static renders, interactive canvases and legends.
- Distribution Stacked Vertical Bars now supports declarative ordered upper-bound buckets through `Buckets < ...` and `Buckets <= ...`, generating `belowN`/`Above` categories with the Tableau throughput palette, a stable legend/stack order and larger horizontal white percentage labels inside segments large enough to contain them in both Canvas and static/PPT renders. The NSA FDTT DL and UL template rows now encode their respective `2,5,20,100` and `1,3,10,20` formulas directly instead of relying on generic numeric ranges.
- Moved the `Re-materialize auto-calculate fields` action from the Auto-calculated Fields introduction into the Materialization status header, next to its current status, so the operation sits with the progress it starts.
- Expanded the Auto-calculated Fields manager to the same wide, content-sized dialog geometry used by Dashboard editing surfaces whether opened from Workspace, the Dashboard viewer or the embedded Report Template editor, mounting the latter above its iframe so it is no longer constrained by the nested viewport. Its height now follows the actual field count without leaving an empty lower area, and the hidden editor can no longer reappear below the manager actions after closing it.
- The Auto-calculated Fields editor uses **Discard** and **Apply** to abandon or stage changes in memory. Its main list enables **Save** and **Save & Materialize** only when definitions have changed; the former persists without starting work, the latter launches background materialization, and either closes the manager after a successful save. Adjacent up/down controls reorder definitions in memory before saving.
- Auto-calculated Field source editors support readable `[Field A] OR [Field B]` aliases, transparently migrate legacy `|` or unbracketed separators, and open a prefix-filtered list of available fields when typing `[`. Their **Rules or Expression** editor supports ordered rules and Tableau-style expressions with parenthesised `AND`/`OR` groups, while retaining legacy semicolons as `AND`.
- The Materialization panel replaces its server-rendered placeholder instead of retaining a second progress bar, reports stopped work at its actual percentage, recognizes cross-worker stop markers immediately and advances inside each table while naming the current table, phase and completed row operations. Long SQLite updates are interruptible within the active statement, while bounded batches reduce transaction overhead.
- The Auto-calculated Fields manager scrolls only when needed, anchors actions at the bottom right, keeps compact controls and a flexible Rules or Expression area, reloads current workspace definitions before opening, and returns to the definition list after applying or discarding so additional fields can be managed in the same session.
- CDF curves retain their complete vertical step when several samples share the automatic minimum. Chart exclusions remove unwanted samples before calculating distributions, and scatter exclusions cover both plotted axes.
- Automatic stacked-bar labels use a consistent horizontal two-decimal format throughout each chart. They select one black or white in-bar text colour from the predominant stack colour and scale up within bounded maxima when the segment or its clear lateral gap has ample space; tiny segments use their segment colour beside the bar and are omitted when that gap or a collision-free vertical position is unavailable.
- Stacked percentage and distribution charts retain every interval when applying their numeric Y domain instead of mixing proportional and pixel accumulators. Older templates remain compatible and gain empty visual-setting columns when next saved.
- Manage Dashboards now exposes editable **NR Mode** and **Template** selectors in every saved Dashboard row. A warning confirmation explains that accepting either change invalidates the Dashboard's prepared reports and charts; the dedicated update preserves Scope, selected CDR universe, date range, adaptive and custom filters, hidden fields and comments, then queues a fresh preparation against the compatible replacement template.
- Dashboard PowerPoint title slides now show the selected `Operator Comparison` or `Vendor Comparison` scope in white below their subtitle at the lower-left, and generated filenames include that scope before the Dashboard name as `timestamp - scope - dashboard.pptx`.
- Admin Dataset Management now stages every row move and filename edit locally, enabling a prominent red **Apply Changes** action aligned at the left immediately above the table only when a draft differs from the saved data. Its adjacent status reports pending name and order edits.
- Applying staged Dataset Management changes queues one cancellable server-side task with validation, per-dataset rename progress and a separate identifier/reference-reordering phase, so the browser does not wait for the operation and progress remains available in the floating background-task panel.
- When creating a Dashboard, its editable name automatically follows the selected template and safely falls back to that template name if left blank.
- Renaming a Report Template updates every Dashboard that references it and replaces that template name in each matching Dashboard title before refreshing its prepared universes.

#### 🐛 Bug fixes:
- The macOS installer now disables application-bundle relocation explicitly, ensuring it installs Dashboard Analytic into `/Applications` even when another copy of the app exists inside the project.
- Applying temporary Chart Definition changes in the individual Dashboard chart editor now renders through a polled background preview job, preventing long combined-CDR queries from being cut off by a reverse proxy with an unexpected 504 response.
- Chart Definition updates repaint the parent slide when the enlarged viewer closes. Update Template and saves from the embedded Report Template editor immediately rebuild the live Dashboard and refresh expanded-chart, restored/PPT and preloaded-editor snapshots, so range, label, filter and exclusion changes no longer remain stale.
- Label Format is preserved by temporary previews and template updates. Visual-only label colour, font, style and size changes reuse the active Canvas model instead of rebuilding the filtered CDR dataset.
- Explicitly closing Chart Definition discards its unapplied draft; Apply and Update Template retain the panel and its draft, outside-click collapse preserves in-progress values, and pointer-leave collapse waits ten seconds.
- Saving a Report Template from the Dashboard viewer and using Refresh Dashboard now invalidate the prior prepared snapshot and rebuild the template structure, so inserted, deleted or reordered slides appear immediately.
- Interactive template saves no longer remain behind an unbounded Workspace writer lock while CDR materialization is running; they return a clear retryable busy response after a short bounded wait.
- Cell Assistance keeps the active `CDR source` cell attached while applying a suggestion, preventing the selected source from being silently discarded. An intentionally empty Legend Position also remains empty instead of being rewritten as `Top`.
- Saving and materializing an Auto-calculated Field records its pending rebuild atomically and updates renamed references across every Report Template and saved Dashboard before starting one background job, avoiding the self-imposed Workspace writer lock that left the editor disabled.
- Large CDR updates commit bounded batches so later field-definition saves can acquire SQLite's writer between batches instead of waiting for a complete table scan.
- The Auto-calculated Field save dialog explains the foreground save and reference migration, then returns to the field list while independently cancellable materialization remains visible in the background-task panel. That panel updates existing job rows in place, keeps each running percentage monotonic and uses stable numeric geometry so bars no longer restart, jump backwards or shake. Orphaned persisted jobs expose the same stop controls and recoverable `stopped` state.
- Dashboard Filters now stays closed when a new authenticated session begins instead of restoring the Dashboard opened by a previous login in the same browser tab. Its visible/hidden and expanded/collapsed choices remain session-only, with the panel expanded by default until the user explicitly collapses it.
- Server transfers to literal private IP addresses now bypass HTTP proxy settings inherited by Docker, preventing LAN requests from being sent through an unrelated proxy. Connection failures also identify the required destination bind address, inbound port and the special `host.docker.internal` case.
- Dashboard PowerPoint generation now isolates a failed chart renderer behind a visible placeholder instead of cancelling the complete presentation, preserves its Canvas model for later inspection, and records both recoverable chart warnings and fatal export errors in App Log with the Dashboard and job identifiers.
- Dashboard Canvas rendering now locates the installed Node runtime by absolute path when launched from the macOS app, whose environment does not inherit the interactive shell `PATH`, so PowerPoint exports retain their charts instead of replacing every chart with a missing-Node placeholder.
- Reordering Operator or Vendor Mappings now invalidates the mapping-dependent chart frames and Canvas payloads of open Dashboards while retaining their prepared universe and token, so the current view immediately follows the saved canonical mapping order instead of retaining a prior source-value order.
- Dashboard comments now follow a stable slide identity rather than the mutable Slide number. Inserting, reordering or deleting Report Template slides preserves each remaining comment on its matching slide, including generated PowerPoint commentary; saved templates automatically migrate legacy numeric keys.
- Stopping an incoming package transfer now uses its correct non-conflicting server endpoint and also interrupts the child materialization it launched, so `Stop all` no longer leaves either task running.
- The Admin Sections navigator now follows the panels' rendered order, including the Import / Export panel positioned directly below the overview.

#### 📚 Documentation:
- Documented workspace-managed Operator/Vendor ordering, thematic chart colours and the combined portable mapping component.

---

## Release: v0.3.2
### Release Date: 2026-09-18
#### ⚠️ Breaking Changes:

#### 🌟 New Features:
- Added a root-level macOS `Dashboard Analytic.app` launcher with the Dashboard Analytic icon and a universal native ARM64/x86_64 executable, plus a standard `.pkg` installer that places it directly in `/Applications`. Its native control window remains in the foreground while the server runs and provides Open in Browser, Restart Server and Stop Server and Quit actions instead of opening the browser automatically; restart waits for the current server to stop cleanly before launching its replacement, while closing or quitting the launcher also stops it and an independent parent-process guard prevents it from remaining orphaned after a forced application termination. The launcher reuses a working project virtual environment or creates its own native environment when existing dependencies are Intel-only, while its PID and log remain outside the app bundle.

#### 🚀 Enhancements:
- Expanded App Logs with meaningful workspace lifecycle and configuration changes plus start, completion, cancellation and failure events for scheduled/manual backups, automatic workspace reconciliation, Auto-calculated Field materialization, combined-CDR recreation, workspace duplication and Dashboard-cache cleanup. System-executed background work preserves the requesting user when available and identifies `system` as its executor.
- Chart Builder now uses the E2E Dashboard individual Canvas chart viewer directly inside the Interactive Preview panel, including its responsive renderer, semantic hover support, zoom, panning and refresh controls, instead of the embedded E2E Reporting PNG preview.
- Reprocess All and multi-file uploads now run as two explicit phases: selected Vendor Mapping datasets may process in parallel, but every CDR in the batch waits until all those mappings finish, ensuring Vendor assignment never uses a partially refreshed mapping set.
- Operator labels are no longer seeded or normalised through built-in UK aliases. Admin Operator Mappings is the sole source for reporting and dashboard label transformations; with no configured mappings, the original CDR labels remain unchanged, including in the combined table.
- Floating background-task cards now keep their workspace title, total task count, top controls and separator fixed while a vertically scrollable task list accommodates processes that exceed the available viewport height, including touch scrolling on compact screens. Tasks retain their immutable queue order oldest-first, new cards start at the top, and two-second status refreshes preserve the same scroll container and the user's current position without treating temporary layout resets as user scrolling or making the scrollbar flicker. Each workspace card also preserves its expanded or minimized state while moving between modules or reloading the page. Browser dataset uploads expose the same Interrupt task control as server jobs and retain their latest measured percentage between timer updates instead of alternating with an indeterminate progress line. Reprocessed datasets calculate queued age from the current queue transition instead of their original upload time.
- Added a configurable application-wide FIFO scheduler for background work. Config now controls the maximum simultaneous tasks (default 1); Dataset processing, mapping, reporting, exports, imports, transfers, backups and workspace maintenance all honour that shared cap, while their visible queued jobs can be stopped cooperatively before or during safe processing stages. Cancelling a backup also removes its partial ZIP and temporary snapshot data.
- Dashboard library actions now place View Dashboard before Open Filters/Close Filters with explicit text labels; compact cards keep both primary actions on one comfortably sized rectangular row, arranging each as icon plus its first word above a contained second word before the remaining icon controls.
- App Log summaries now begin with their configured-local timestamp in `[yyyy-mm-dd HH:MM:SS]` format.
- Dashboard dataset preparation tasks now remain individual entries in their workspace background-task card, with the Dashboard name included directly in each task label rather than creating Dashboard subgroups.
- Dashboard Dataset Universe counts now reuse materialized CDR row counts when complete and unfiltered; reduced (CDR/date) and filtered universes otherwise share one narrow aggregate query per combined table, with both count sets cached independently from adaptive filter catalogues.
- Config Runtime Settings now consistently lays out its Locale, Chart Rendering, Data Processing and Background Work cards in two columns on desktop.
- Opening a saved Dashboard now immediately hydrates its stored Dataset Universe and filter selections in the controls, even while background preparation is still loading the current catalogues and counts; an older browser-session universe can no longer override an explicitly saved one.
- Once an open Dashboard reaches Ready, its available chart models are warmed sequentially during browser idle time even before View Dashboard is opened, so the viewer can reuse the cached chart payloads without competing with active interaction.
- New and previously unprepared Dashboards now automatically cache four date-unrestricted universes using their saved filters, scope and template: every ready CDR of each type, the newest CDR, the two newest CDRs, and the newest plus the third-newest CDR. Every universe uses automatic `Oldest`/`Newest` dates. Manage Dashboards reports `Preparing n/4` until they are reusable while keeping **Open Filters** and **View Dashboard** enabled; an explicit open takes priority over the low-priority warm-up. Status polling now lets that background work finish instead of cancelling it between polls, so its persisted manifests are restored after restart rather than repeatedly returning to `Preparing 0/4`. Contended work reliably resumes after interactive or PowerPoint preparation. The last opened Dashboard is restored whenever E2E Dashboard is revisited or reloaded in the same signed-in browser tab, until it is explicitly closed or the user logs out.
- Ready PowerPoint Generation Jobs now provide a View Dashboard action immediately after View Charts. Its calendar-and-check SVG opens the job's immutable generated Dashboard snapshot—including its original slide layout, Canvas chart models and comments—without recalculating data or reapplying filters; Generate PPT and other mutating controls are disabled there, while View Dataset remains available and reconstructs each chart's dataset from the job's stored definition and filters.
- Generate PPT now inserts its job immediately, including for a closed or non-prewarmed Dashboard. Cache lookup, universe preparation and chart-model rendering run only after that insertion in the background worker, which first reuses a compatible persistent snapshot when one exists.
- Cold PowerPoint jobs now report progress across the real preparation (1–24%), Canvas chart-model rendering (25–90%), chart/PPT composition (91–95%) and final PPT write (96–100%) phases instead of appearing stuck at 1% until rendering ends; a chart whose stored template field is absent from a selected CDR is exported as an explanatory placeholder without failing the complete PPT job.
- Server access and execution output now uses the configured-local `[yyyy-mm-dd HH:MM:SS]` timestamp prefix and is retained in a live, scrollable Execution Log panel beneath App Logs. Production and development Docker services use the same Python launcher and logging configuration, so `docker compose logs` retains that timestamp instead of beginning at Uvicorn's `INFO:` prefix.
- Collapsed side-navigation tabs now recede into subtle edge shadows, revealing their complete Modules, Sections, Documentation or Releases handle only when the pointer approaches the corresponding viewport edge. Revealed handles and open drawers hide automatically after five seconds without pointer or keyboard focus, reset that delay on interaction and close immediately when the page scrolls or loses focus. On touch screens, tapping the left or right edge reveals the available handles for the same five-second interval before they are opened.
- Dashboard slide, Dashboard expanded-chart and E2E Reporting chart viewer First/Last navigation actions now use the same crisp line-based SVG language as the other paging controls instead of platform-dependent emoji glyphs.
- PowerPoint generation from a Dashboard library row now uses a single `Select Dashboard Datasets Universe` dialog with ordered Scope, CDR datasets and Dates sections before its Cancel and Generate PPT actions. Changing Scope refreshes the CDR checkboxes, preselecting the two newest datasets of each type for Operator Comparison or the newest one for Multivendor Comparison; Dates initially reflects that Dashboard's saved concrete or automatic Oldest/Newest range, and the export preserves the complete explicit universe. On compact screens, bounded per-type CDR lists, two-column date controls and correctly sized fields keep the panel within the viewport, its central content independently scrollable and its actions always visible.
- View Dashboard from the Dashboard library now opens the viewer without expanding the main filter panel. Viewer and individual-chart actions consistently call it Dashboard Filters, and its floating panel omits the redundant View Dashboard and Generate PPT controls. The main Dashboard Filters panel starts collapsed for every new authenticated session, while its open or closed state survives reloads and module navigation only within that same session. Discarding applied but unsaved filters or Dataset Universe changes while leaving restores their saved values, including the session-retained universe, and filter preparation retries one transient proxy 501 response. Every action in the viewer's upper row combines an SVG icon with text at a consistent size; the orange Generate PPT action appears immediately before the red Refresh Dashboard action at the end. Presentation now leads the slide-navigation row and is separated from First by a spaced vertical divider, while presentation mode retains separate floating Pause/Resume and Stop controls.
- PowerPoint Generation Jobs and Charts Panel now rely on the E2E Dashboard stack's single consistent inter-panel gap instead of adding their own top margins; the movable Dashboard Filters host also disappears from the grid while its panel is hidden or floating, preventing an empty row from creating extra whitespace.
- Selecting Full Environment in Admin Import / Export / Transfer no longer opens the workspace picker immediately. The picker now appears only after pressing Export ZIP or Transfer to other server, and each action requests its own current workspace selection before it starts.
- The release utility is now `CreateUpdateRelease.py`, with separate Update Current Release and Create New Release actions. It can create a version-named local branch after confirming how to handle pending work, add an empty dated changelog release, update application metadata, commit the result and publish the new branch to `origin` automatically.

#### 🐛 Bug fixes:
- App Logs now renders timestamps and derives date filters with the timezone selected in Application Runtime instead of relying on the host process timezone, preventing UTC offsets and midnight events from appearing under the wrong local date.
- CDR processing with explicitly selected Vendor Mappings now fails visibly when mapping raises an error or leaves any Vendor value empty, instead of publishing an apparently successful Ready dataset without complete mapped Vendors.
- The Dashboard viewer now uses compact layouts on small screens: landscape reduces its header and uses single-row selection/actions plus a combined tool/navigation row, while portrait also combines playback/navigation without clipping, compacts editing controls and expands the proportionally positioned chart composition through all remaining viewport height instead of stacking oversized fixed-height charts or leaving unused space. Its collapsible Comments drawer stays fixed to the visible bottom edge and device safe area in landscape independently of overflowing slide content, instead of appearing in the middle of the viewer. The Presentation settings dialog is constrained to the visible viewport, scrolls vertically with touch momentum and keeps its header and actions reachable. Across compact modules, Collapse/Expand stays on the title row at the right, and Charts Panel uses a smaller chart-count badge that does not overlap its title.
- Configuration and Full Environment imports now preserve destination-local server-transfer handshakes while replacing the application database, preventing transfer status polling from briefly returning Not Found and aborting an otherwise successful incoming import.

#### 📚 Documentation:

---

## Release: v0.3.1
### Release Date: 2026-09-15
#### ⚠️ Breaking Changes:
- Removed `Report_Vendor` from individual and combined CDR tables. Normalization version 11 migrates its values into the official `Vendor` field, which now drives filters, legends, analysis, Dashboards and Reporting.

#### 🌟 New Features:
- Dataset Preview now pages the complete persisted dataset in fixed groups of 100 rows and every unified Dataset table preserves its exact horizontal column position when changing pages.
- Added simultaneous Excel-style column filters with complete, cascading value lists and `Select All/None`.
- Added comma-separated column search and multi-select label filtering with an `All Labels` option.
- Added searchable Workspace Dataset selection with a loading dialog directly in Dataset Preview.
- Added persisted, provenance-based `CDR-Main`, `Vendor-Map`, `Derived`, `Auto-calculated`, `Analysis-derived` and CDR-type badges with rule details and distinct green shades for main and derived CDR fields, including darker `CDR-Main` headers for clear separation. Physical headers are catalogued once per dataset in the Workspace database instead of reopening source CDRs for each preview; `Vendor` reflects mapping provenance and `Vendor_Only` remains derived.
- Added the `PINNED` badge for fields retained across dataset views.
- Ordered previews as origin, main CDR, Auto-calculated, remaining derived and source CDR fields.
- Added compact filter clearing, thousands-separated row totals and round green SVG pagination controls.
- Added a dedicated workspace Operator Mappings panel directly below Report Templates Management, preloading every existing mapping and grouping each editable canonical label with all its editable source aliases, with seeded NetCheck aliases applied only to chart data and chart-template filters. Operator Mappings can now be exported, imported or transferred independently between selected workspaces and selected as their own granular component in on-demand/scheduled Backup and Restore packages.
- Added shared fixed module navigators to every authenticated main module, themed automatically with the active module's colour palette. The left-side `Modules` tab sits above the lower `Sections` tab and provides grouped Main Modules, Administrative Modules and Documentation links, marks the current module and keeps unavailable workspace or role-restricted modules visibly disabled; its larger drawer accommodates the complete module list. `Sections` starts with `Main Menu`, includes a trailing home SVG and returns to the top of the page; its remaining list tracks only currently rendered top-level panels, highlights the current viewport section and scrolls directly to the selected panel before collapsing again. It dynamically follows panels being shown, hidden, inserted, removed or moved—including Dashboard Datasets & Filters and both Reporting/Dashboard Charts Panels—and always performs a fresh visibility scan when opened. Embedded editors, previews and fixed sidebar controls such as Analysis Controls remain excluded; each introductory header normally uses its upper eyebrow label, documentation pages use their second document-title line, Workspace presents `Workspaces Management` above `Select Workspace`, Dataset Analysis shows the selected dataset name or `Select Dataset`, Chart Builder presents `Chart Builder` above `Ad-Hoc Analysis` and gives its second `Interactive Preview` panel a visible heading plus a `Chart Definition` editor label, and App Logs retains `Application activity` above `App Logs`.

#### 🚀 Enhancements:
- Dashboard caches remain valid across patch releases when their data formats do not change.
- Dashboard Refresh confirms, invalidates and rebuilds every chart model in the active preview.
- Refresh Chart invalidates and rebuilds only the current expanded chart.
- Dashboard preview identities now include the complete Report Template content.
- Relaunched Dashboard PPT Jobs rebuild charts and slides from the current edited template while retaining the recorded dataset universe and filters.
- Manage Dashboards asks for Operator or Multivendor Comparison before queueing PPT generation.
- Automatic PPT universes use the newest eligible CDRs and their full available date range.
- PowerPoint Generation Jobs shows five recent rows before scrolling.
- Expanded charts support previous and next navigation with larger, widely separated buttons, arrow keys or horizontal touch gestures: swiping left advances and swiping right returns in Dashboard, expanded individual-chart and Reporting chart viewers, while zoomed charts retain their own pan gesture. E2E Dashboard matches Reporting's chart-navigation icons and spacing in its own purple palette; its individual-chart viewer now also uses Reporting's full-screen card composition, descriptive header, inset canvas and red Close control while retaining the floating Chart Definition panel. On compact screens, individual Dashboard and Reporting chart titles use their own row, wrap long text without clipping and keep actions in a separate row; the Dashboard individual viewer keeps its red Close control beside the title in that upper row. The primary View Dashboard action occupies a larger full-width row. Immediately before the chart area, the viewer centers Presentation, Refresh and Generate PPT in one row followed by its centered slide-navigation row, with larger and more widely separated touch targets; its Comments panel remains after the charts, collapsed by default and expandable, while editing actions stay separate. View Dashboard shares Reporting's red Close control and reserves the subtitle height on slides without one. Presentation mode dedicates the complete viewport to the slide and its correctly positioned charts, hides headers, comments, navigation and editing controls, retains only a floating Stop control and lets Escape return to the standard viewer. Both its live and expanded charts provide boundary-aware left, right, up and down panning arrows whenever zoom is active, while View Dashboard and the expanded viewer stay above floating background-task cards. Reporting chart canvases expose a larger floating top-right filtered-dataset icon followed by zoom controls on hover, then hide the controls three seconds after the pointer leaves. On touch screens, tapping a Dashboard or individual chart temporarily reveals View Dataset, Refresh and other non-zoom floating actions while keeping zoom hidden; the individual viewer does not reveal Chart Definition merely because View Dataset receives focus, and Reporting continues hiding its complete floating canvas group.
- Added a collapsible Chart Definition panel to live and generated-PPT charts.
- Chart Definition Apply renders the complete edited definition and materializes a newly requested field in the shared combined CDR table only when required.
- Update Template renders the current chart first and persists it to its exact template row in one confirmed action.
- Improved hierarchical chart panes, aggregation labels, separators and bottom-legend spacing.
- E2E Dashboards restores its scroll position after reload and asynchronous layout updates.
- Updated `Template_CDR_analysis.pptx` styling.
- Unified field-name resolution across ingestion, analysis, SQL queries, charts and previews.
- Field references now ignore case and separators; `Subscriber` and legacy `Suscriber` resolve identically.
- Campaign filters accept reordered country, year, quarter and SA/NSA tokens while preserving source values.
- Normalized Benchmark, Period, Market, geography, technology, session, result and event-time field families.
- Added `Vendor_Only` after `Vendor` in Datasets Analysis Adaptive Filters.
- The Vendors metric now counts distinct `Vendor_Only` values and follows Operators.
- Global outcome cards use Tests for Data and Calls for Voice/Speech; Completed counts the filtered total and Dropped Calls follows Failed for every CDR type.
- Every background task shows a live execution timer in the floating task card, including client-side dataset uploads and the completed state. Queued tasks now show `Queued Nm ago` without accruing execution time; their real timer starts only when processing begins. This separation applies consistently to Dataset ingestion and Vendor processing, explicitly requested E2E Dashboard preparation and PowerPoint rendering, Reports, Chart Sets, Auto-calculated Field materialization, imports, exports, transfers, backups, restores and Workspace maintenance. Persisted jobs retain their execution-only duration through dedicated start/finish timestamps, while process-owned jobs keep equivalent queue/start timestamps in their live task state. A card remembers its minimized state only while its current tasks remain visible and always reappears expanded after disappearing and later receiving new work. Floating-panel Stop All interrupts queued tasks first and then running tasks sequentially, preventing concurrent Workspace writes from leaving part of the batch active; it suppresses false per-task completion cards and reports one consolidated result after every cancellation request has completed.
- CDR previews mark every field as `PINNED` or `UN_PINNED`; mapping previews hide empty optional and Analysis-derived fields while retaining metadata, Region, GCID, Operator and Technology_Primary.
- Added application-wide Config settings for timezone, chart renderer, Chromium and event-time filtering, including searchable IANA timezones; event-time bypass disables only dataset date-range prefilters with an explanatory tooltip.
- Background task cards can be moved and restored to their default dock position with a target control.
- Dataset file uploads run without a blocking page overlay and report byte progress plus elapsed time in the floating background-task card; processing continues after logout or Workspace changes and automatically resumes after an application restart. Import-time VFUK/3UK mapping selections are persisted and reused by retries, legacy lock recovery and explicit reprocessing, while clearing Vendors deliberately clears those selections.
- Unified CDR and filtered chart dataset viewers across Dashboards, PPT snapshots, Reporting, Chart Sets and Report Template editing, with column counts and lazy cached filter loading.
- CDR and chart dataset viewers now provide right-aligned filtered CSV export; chart dialogs use full-height 5%-inset layouts, light badge hover states and consistent close controls.
- Combined CDR tables now permanently materialize the fixed Dataset Preview fields, every E2E Dashboard Default Filter alias and every KPI referenced by any saved workspace Report Template. A versioned contract backfills existing tables only when their required columns change. Dashboard preparation no longer validates or repairs columns per selected CDR universe—and does not enter `Preparing Dashboard source columns` when only filter values change: alternative Default Filter aliases that are legitimately absent from one physical CDR schema and Auto-calculated Fields assigned to a different CDR type no longer produce false missing-column detections. Filter values query the shared combined source by `dataset_id`, with their exact SQL row counts and catalogues reused from the persistent filter-state cache when revisiting the same selection. Only a newly requested Dashboard-specific field absent from its applicable source can extend a combined table; that extension is applied to every ready CDR of the relevant type so later dataset-selection changes remain SQL-only.
- Dashboard Dataset & Filters and the floating background-task card now show the same precise live preparation phase: waiting for a worker slot, validating the template and CDR sources, checking, reading or restoring Dataset Universe date limits, checking or restoring the filtered-selection cache, counting each CDR type in its combined table, loading or calculating filter options, caching the result, preparing slide positions and saving the reusable Dashboard cache. Indeterminate and queued phases no longer display a misleading synthetic `0%`. The main preparation request is dispatched before its progress polling begins, superseded requests leave the shared preparation gate immediately, and combined-table count/facet queries no longer hold the task-registry lock that progress polling needs; a long SQL scan therefore reports its real phase instead of remaining incorrectly at `Waiting for the server to register`. Dataset Universe changes now have dedicated SVG `Apply Universe`, `Save Universe` and saved-value Reload actions directly below their panel: Apply uses Scope, CDR and date changes without persisting them, while Save stores that universe with the Dashboard. Filter actions remain isolated below the right-hand filter panels, and Generate PPT plus View Dashboard occupy the following row. Leaving the module resolves unsaved filter and universe changes independently, while logout clears their temporary browser-session state.
- Dataset Preview links now ask whether to open in a new tab or the current tab. New tabs display a Dataset-loading dialog until navigation completes, while the current-tab option uses a floating viewer inset 2% from every browser edge, with the same compact red × close action as the other floating panels plus backdrop and Escape dismissal. Escape remains reliable after focus moves into the embedded preview or its Dataset selector. Its embedded preview omits the application header, module tabs and background-task cards, and shows its own loading dialog until the Dataset is ready.
- Removed the materialized Dashboard row-key cache, the auxiliary `dashboard-analytics.sqlite3` projection database and every automatic Dashboard/Canvas warm-up triggered by workspace startup, library listing, Dashboard saving, template-only edits, cache clearing or unrelated exports. E2E Dashboard filters, chart datasets and rendering now query the indexed combined CDR tables directly; only newly requested missing fields are materialized there, explicit open/refresh/PPT actions prepare their own selection, chart models render on demand, valid manifests remain reusable across restarts, and clearing cache leaves it empty until the next requested operation. While the Dashboard viewer is open, its silent client-side preloader waits for the visible slide and then caches neighbouring slides in alternating forward/backward distance order; the individual-chart viewer applies the same order to adjacent charts. Navigation restarts the sequence around the new position, closing the viewer stops it, and this opportunistic work never appears as a floating background task.
- The Dashboard Filters panel heading uses `DASHBOARD FILTERS: DASHBOARD NAME`, renders the slightly larger uppercase name in the same typography with a contrasting dark-green bold weight, follows in-place renames, returns to its neutral label when closed and replaces the redundant Dashboard badge beside Collapse. The panel starts collapsed in a new browser session and retains the user's open or closed choice across reloads and module changes only for the remainder of that session.
- E2E Dashboard starts with every Dashboard closed after a fresh module entry or a new login, while a browser reload restores the Dashboard that was open in that page; View Dashboard is the first control in each library row, followed by the prominent orange Open Filters action that exposes its dataset and filter controls.
- E2E Dashboard now restores its saved vertical scroll position only after refreshing the page; entering through a module tab, browser history navigation or any other fresh navigation clears the stored position and starts at the top.
- Moved the shared Materialization status card from Auto-calculated Fields to the end of the Workspace Datasets table, where it reports work affecting both individual and combined CDRs and shows every simultaneous active or queued materialization as an independent labelled message and progress bar. Its summary now distinguishes the real `N in progress` count from `N queued`—including `0 in progress` while work is waiting—while manual combined-CDR recreations use a dedicated serial queue rather than being starved by file-ingestion or Vendor-mapping workers. A recreation remains queued until it owns its exclusive workspace locks, and queued 0% work no longer receives a synthetic visible bar. The floating task card likewise shows `Queued Nm ago` without a running timer or progress bar until execution begins. The table now displays comma-grouped Rows and Columns for individual and combined datasets, and refreshes its counts, status, progress and Updated value in place when materialization finishes without reloading the page. Combined CDR rows sit below a prominent separator and concise explanation identifying them as generated read-only query sources that are created from ready individual CDRs rather than uploaded or imported separately, followed by their own repeated column header.
- Improved narrow-screen navigation and readability across the application: compact layouts now cover phones and split-screen windows up to 640 px, both module-tab rows fit their viewport and remain separate when a phone rotates to landscape, administrative Operator Mappings and the Dashboard library render as labelled cards with client-side card paging, small nested KPI cards share rows, and action groups prefer compact single-line controls instead of oversized full-width buttons. Dashboard management cards place Actions beside Status, with larger Open/Close and View controls on their first action row and the four remaining controls below. Dense introductions, panels—including the Dashboard manager—dialogs, Dashboard filters, comments and expanded-chart controls remain compact and contained within the mobile viewport; the Auto-calculated Fields JSON chooser and Import action retain matching dimensions on one row, Dashboard PPT job cards place their double-width Dashboard name beside Scope and Status beside a double-width Progress field, while Reporting job cards place a double-width Template beside Type and Datasets beside Status instead of reserving complete rows, show the Dataset action below its column label and keep both Delete All actions together on one row. Editable controls retain a 16 px mobile font to prevent iOS focus zoom, while data matrices that require column comparison remain horizontally scrollable inside their own viewport.
- Added Dashboard Analytic branding to the upper-right corner of every chart in the on-screen Dashboard viewer, without exporting it to generated PowerPoint files. The larger pointer-inert logo is rendered without a shadow or background box and dynamically aligns with the chart title.
- Standardized the rendered position of Dashboard chart titles across every chart type and aspect ratio, keeping titles and their aligned viewer-only logos at the same screen coordinates instead of allowing canvas scaling to move them between charts.
- Refined the collapsible Dashboard Comments area for both bottom and right-hand layouts. The right-hand panel starts level with the chart rather than the navigation controls, uses approximately one quarter of the available width when expanded, leaves the chart three quarters, and collapses to a narrow clickable vertical label with a clearer arrow. The bottom layout uses the same chart separation, and both variants share a pastel-purple treatment.
- Kept the Dashboard Presentation, Refresh, Generate PPT and slide-navigation controls centred across the complete viewer width even when the right-hand Comments panel is expanded, so opening comments never displaces either control row.
- Extended Presentation settings with an independent editable transition duration from 0.1 to 10 seconds, defaulting to one second, while retaining a separate editable interval for how long each slide remains visible.
- Expanded Dashboard Presentation transitions with Fade, Slide from right, Slide from left, Zoom, Rise, Blur, Soft rotate, Flip, Bounce, Wipe, Mosaic, Curtain, Blinds and None. None and Random appear first, followed by a separated alphabetical list of effects; Flip is the default and the last selected mode persists between browser sessions. Random includes every animated effect and avoids immediately repeating the previous random transition.
- Added an optional Presentation setting for slide comments. When enabled, only slides containing comments show them, using the same Comments presentation as the standard Dashboard viewer; when disabled, the chart continues to use the complete presentation area.
- Improved Dashboard Presentation controls: every run starts on slide 1, Pause/Resume and Stop remain visible together in the upper-right corner, Space or F8 toggles Pause/Resume, F8 starts Presentation from the Dashboard viewer, F7 returns to the previous slide and F9 advances to the next slide. Arrow keys and horizontal swipe navigation remain available during Presentation without stopping it.
- Removed implicit NSA/SA and NR Mode sample-selection rules from Dashboard counting, filtering and chart-data generation. Dashboard RAT membership is now controlled exclusively by the user through the Dashboard's general filters, preventing hidden sample exclusions from changing counts or chart results.
- Accelerated Dashboard filtering by recalculating only the filtered-universe count when ordinary filters change, consolidating adaptive-facet reads into grouped SQL passes and applying normalized, date and selection predicates directly to indexed combined-CDR queries used by chart generation.
- Rebalanced the Dashboard Dataset Universe and Dataset Filters panels so both columns finish at the same height, the wider Universe panel keeps Apply Universe, Save Universe and Reload Universe on one line, and filter actions stay left-aligned in their own column. Preparation and ready notices remain at the bottom, followed by centred Generate PPT and View Dashboard actions; the PPT action now has its own distinct colour.
- Standardized Workspace and Admin Dataset-table actions so every individual row keeps the same ordered controls and disables actions that do not apply to its type or processing state. Vendor Mapping now uses a linked-records SVG and Clear Vendor Mapping uses an eraser SVG; trailing Admin actions also render Reprocess, Stop and Delete as SVG-only controls rather than exposing their text labels. The position immediately after Clear Vendor Mapping shows source-file Reprocess for ready, failed and stopped datasets, but switches exclusively to Stop while processing; after stopping, Reprocess becomes available again. Clear Vendor Mapping uses the previous amber Stop/Reprocess palette, while Stop/Reprocess use its previous pink palette. Delete Dataset always appears last. A compact bulk-action row is available above both Workspace and Admin Dataset tables and follows the same relative action order, SVGs and colours with Map All, Clear All, Reprocess All, Stop All and the destructive Remove All action last; in Workspace it remains vertically aligned with the Dataset Type selector. Map All and Clear All open multi-CDR selectors with every eligible row selected; Reprocess All opens a floating confirmation panel with every available source-backed dataset selected by default, preserves mapping-before-CDR dependencies and rebuilds each selection from its original raw file; Stop All confirms and stops every queued or actively processing dataset, suppresses per-dataset stop warnings and reports one completion message after the complete batch has stopped; Remove All confirms and permanently deletes every dataset and original source file, remaining disabled until processing datasets are stopped. The Workspace Dataset table starts with IDs descending and allows its individual dataset rows to be reordered by clicking ID, Dataset, Input Type, Rows, Columns, Size, Status, Progress, Uploaded or Updated, retaining the chosen order during live status refreshes while combined CDR tables remain in their dedicated lower section. Admin rows also include palette-matched SVG Move Up and Move Down controls in a dedicated leading Order column; they reorder datasets by their visible position, renumber IDs contiguously and atomically propagate the new IDs through profiles, source-column metadata, materialised tables, combined reporting rows, generated jobs, saved Dashboard definitions, processing options and audit data, showing the shared floating progress dialog while this potentially long update completes. Adjacent moves now touch only the two changed IDs, preserve existing materialised-table indexes and update non-unique references in a single set-based SQL pass instead of renumbering and reindexing every dataset. Completing a move refreshes only the Dataset Management table in place, preserves the current page scroll and synchronizes dependent Admin controls instead of reloading the full page. Renaming a dataset also uses the shared floating progress dialog while its file, path and materialised references are updated. Dataset Management displays IDs from highest to lowest and preserves that visible order after a move; movement is disabled while ingestion is queued or processing. Database Management labels the corresponding selector group simply as Individual Datasets and orders its materialised tables by numeric dataset ID from highest to lowest.

#### 🐛 Bug fixes:
- Removed legacy `__N` collision columns while retaining genuine duplicate source headers as `_Duplicate_N`.
- Fixed Dataset Preview badge alignment so hidden columns no longer leave stacked badges, while preserving semantic colours inside themed modules.
- Excluded timestamp fields from analysis metrics; expired sessions stop passive polling without redirecting the current page or repeatedly returning unauthorized responses.
- Task polling uses short-lived SQLite reads, registry handles close deterministically, size scans are cached and login skips repeated Workspace migrations. Workspace databases now enable WAL once during initialization, ordinary requests no longer change connection safety settings, and every Repository transaction shares a per-workspace write coordinator while read, parsing, analysis, Dataset processing and PPT-rendering phases remain parallel. Dataset workers retain parallelism for ordinary files but admit only one 256 MB-or-larger source per workspace at a time, preventing simultaneous pandas materializations from exhausting memory and making the web interface unavailable. Dashboard selection scans use short read-only queries against the shared combined CDR tables. This prevents E2E Dashboard preparation from colliding with Dataset ingestion, Vendor mapping/clearing, Auto-calculated Field materialization or combined-CDR recreation; legacy lock recovery queues without writing from the Workspace request, so a busy database can no longer turn that page into an Internal Server Error, and failed mappings leave their source CDR ready for use and remapping.
- Background task interruption remains successful when its non-critical audit entry encounters a temporary SQLite write lock.
- Fixed dataset-dialog loading, Escape dismissal and E2E filter layering. Reporting filtered-dataset panels now open immediately while retaining the floating Loading Filtered Dataset dialog, fill their parent viewer with a 2% inset on every side, and reuse one cached source frame across charts from the same Chart Set and CDR type while projecting each chart's own KPI and filter columns instead of rebuilding almost-identical SQLite/Pandas frames.
- Database Viewer now opens any remaining `WITHOUT ROWID` internal tables as safely paginated, filterable, read-only data instead of failing with an invalid JSON error.
- Kept an active or closed Dashboard marked `Ready` after restoring or preparing its session-specific Dataset Universe: polling now validates the unchanged universe remembered for that Dashboard instead of checking the saved definition's unrelated default universe, incorrectly reverting to `Open to prepare` and disabling its PowerPoint actions.
