Both the Active Dashboard and library-row View actions remain disabled throughout dataset preparation and are enabled together only when the prepared view is available.
- Multi-selection filter menus now remain open after `Select All / None`, allowing users to review or refine the resulting selection without repeatedly reopening the dropdown; outside-click, single-choice and configured delayed closing behaviours remain unchanged.
- Returning to a previously prepared Dashboard filter combination now validates its remembered preview token before reuse and transparently restores the persistent manifest when that token has expired. Passive status polling validates manifests without materializing throwaway snapshots, preventing it from evicting active previews, and Refresh Dashboard now recovers an expired preview before invalidating and rebuilding its charts instead of repeating `Dashboard preview expired` on every chart.
- Preserved source `Operator` values in individual and combined materialized CDR tables, Dataset Preview column filters, Dataset Analysis selectors and E2E Dashboard filters. Canonical workspace mappings are now applied only to temporary chart frames and chart-template filters; chart-dataset tables and their Operator, Subscriber, Vendor and Vendor_Only dropdowns now use that same mapped frame, so a visible canonical value is always selectable. Changing a mapping invalidates chart caches without rematerializing CDRs. Vendor Mapping uses the fast materialized-table path normally and rebuilds a CDR from its original source only when its normalization version predates 13, preventing an old canonicalized materialization from being written back. Normalization version 13 rebuilds version-11 and version-12 CDRs once from their original source to recover values previously overwritten during ingestion, including tables that a Vendor-only operation had incorrectly marked as migrated without reloading their source; Vendor mapping no longer advances the normalization version. Recreating a combined Data, Voice or Speech table performs that recovery for every affected individual CDR of the same type in its background job before rebuilding the combined table, with per-table progress; the combined-preview Loading Dataset panel also explains this all-table compatibility migration.

#### 📚 Documentation:
- Reorganized Workspace Management help under NetCheck CDR Support with one rule per fixed field.
- Documented CDR normalization, Campaign parsing, ISO dates and chart-only Operator Mappings.
- Documented Preview ordering, label filtering, rule tooltips and `PINNED` fields.
- Expanded VFUK and 3UK Vendor Mapping formats, GCID rules and Vendor assignment behavior.
- Marked Smart Orchestrator Logs as listed but not yet supported for ingestion and analysis.

---

## Release: v0.3.0
### Release Date: 2026-09-14
#### ⚠️ Breaking Changes:
- Renamed former `E2E Dashboard` module to `Datasets Analysis`, including its controls, internal analysis references and canonical `/datasets-analysis` routes; legacy `/dashboard` routes remain available as compatibility aliases. Help chapters now place E2E Dashboards immediately before E2E Reporting.
- Renamed the E2E Reporting page, API, generated report, Chart Set and job routes to the canonical `/e2e-reporting` and `/api/e2e-reporting` prefixes. Legacy `/reporting` links redirect to the new location.

#### 🌟 New Features:
- Added E2E Dashboards with workspace-persisted Dashboards, create/open/save/close/duplicate/delete and JSON import/export, all workspace templates, combined CDR selections, adaptive date/dimension filters and custom Auto-calculated Field value filters. Its large viewer groups all charts by Slide using template Layout positions, with navigation, synchronized floating filters, filtered chart datasets with cached partial-row pagination, compact CSV download icons, template editing and the shared Auto-calculated Field manager.
- Added persistent per-slide Dashboard comments with editable bullet-style notes in an independently scrolling area placed below the charts or at their right according to the selected Layout, plus Presentation mode with selectable 3/5/10/15-second automatic transitions and Fade, Slide or no effect.

#### 🚀 Enhancements:
- Reused shared materialized CDR tables and Reporting chart preparation for Dashboards, with consistent filtered snapshots, debounced changes and stale-response protection. Dashboard preparation now calculates exact filtered row counts and filter catalogues for every selection; small selections retain indexed row keys, while large selections use direct SQL predicates without copying their complete CDRs. A bounded, revision-keyed analytical SQLite cache stores only the fields required by each Dashboard/template, is built when Dashboard preparation needs it and is rebuilt only when its datasets or required columns change, so management operations never wait for large projection copies. Each live chart reads its minimal filtered projection and returns a compact JSON model with bounded CDF/scatter samples or pre-aggregated bars/tables. The browser paints it from a 1600 × 900 logical canvas using the historical Report/Chart Set renderer's row/column hierarchy, aggregation and campaign ordering, Operator/Vendor/Campaign palettes, titles, legend content and position, line emphasis and semantic hover values; Map charts also share its OpenStreetMap viewport and load tiles asynchronously. Reports, Chart Sets and previews now use that Canvas painter by default, share Dashboard-style filtered frames and persist its in-paint semantic hits instead of recalculating tooltip geometry in Python; the legacy PIL painter remains available through `DASHBOARD_ANALYTIC_REPORT_CHART_RENDERER=pil`. The renderer stretches each chart independently to fill its complete template placeholder, centres its visible Rendering state and supports independent 100–400% chart zoom with drag-to-pan; Title and Transition dashboards use large typography plus the Dashboard Analytic logo and title. Exact chart models persist across restarts; saved Dashboards warm every Canvas model through a bounded server queue, while an applied Dashboard selection becomes usable as soon as its compact projections are ready and leaves per-chart frame loading to that rendering queue. Repeated charts share their filtered frame. Shared-CDR repair validation checks every requested field in at most one target and one source scan per dataset instead of rescanning large CDRs once per empty column. Docker multi-platform builds keep their large Chromium dependency cache in Docker Hub and cancel superseded duplicate builds; tag releases invoke this workflow only through Build All, avoiding repeated ARM64 emulation. Added incremental shared-CDR column backfills, deterministic invalidation, a distinct muted-violet theme, responsive template layouts, and a pastel-yellow preparation notice that turns pastel green when View Dashboard is available.
- Relaunching an interrupted Report or Chart Set now verifies and reuses its completed PNG charts and tooltip JSON files, removes incomplete or obsolete artifacts, and generates only the remaining charts; a deliberate relaunch of a completed job still starts clean.
- Made E2E Dashboards available to every authenticated user with access to the active workspace, matched its inactive tab styling to the other E2E module tabs while retaining its distinct violet active state, and expanded the dashboard viewer to a 96%-viewport 16:9 slide canvas so template chart layouts use the available screen area. Dashboard management now selects NR Mode beside its name and filters the Template list accordingly; its library is a collapsible table with a high-visibility total badge and compact white folder-open, white exit-door, duplicate, export and delete actions per dashboard; the active viewer places Refresh and Presentation icons after navigation and aligns Adaptative Filters, Auto-Calculated Fields and Edit Template at the right; Escape closes the Auto-Calculated Fields and Template Editor dialogs when they contain no unsaved changes. Import Dashboard clearly restores a previously exported Dashboard. Admin Dashboard transfers and imports now use the same destination-workspace picker and source-workspace preselection as Report Templates, with a server-side name-match fallback when an older client does not return the picker selection. The collapsible Dashboard Datasets & Filters panel identifies the open Dashboard and gives Apply, Save, Clear, Reload, Close and Add Filter their own explanatory SVG icons, with larger SVGs for Generate PPT and View Dashboard. Its two columns are explicitly headed Select Dataset Universe and Select Dataset Filters. Clear Filters and Reload Saved Filters remain beside Save Filters and Apply Filters; Apply and Save enable only for real filter changes and never for universe-only changes. Reopening the same Dashboard or returning after a page reload reuses its valid in-memory or server snapshot without preparing again; expired snapshots and user-applied changes show the preparation card. Its larger Scope label and button-height selector sit directly beside the highlighted View Dashboard action. Changing Scope now rebuilds the temporary Dataset Universe immediately without a dialog, selecting the latest CDR of every type for Multivendor and the two most recent CDRs of every type for Operator Comparison. Scope, CDRs and dates are session-only universe controls, never produce unapplied/unsaved-filter badges and are removed from every persisted Dashboard definition during migration. View Dashboard opens immediately after a universe change, centres the yellow Preparing Dashboard dataset notice and replaces it automatically with the updated slides. Hover tooltips plus filtered row totals appear in the ready card, and its floating filter panel gives the filter-application guidance distinct spacing below the actions, cannot be collapsed, and changes preparation/ready messages to refer to the Dashboard instead of its unavailable View Dashboard action; it uses floating Dataset Analysis-style multiselects split into Default Filters and a distinct pastel-pink Additional Filters subpanel. Its default filters remove Technology completely, resolve Region through `Region`, `G_Level_2` or `G Level 2` and City through `City`, `G_Level_4` or `G Level 4`, skipping empty higher-priority geographic values row by row; they place Campaign and RAT immediately after City, use the same `RAT_A`, `RAT`, `Sample_RAT_A` precedence as Dataset Preview, and include Call Status. Filters with aliases show a hover and keyboard-focus tooltip listing every supported column in priority order. A versioned workspace migration replaces Zone with Region in every saved Dashboard and restores the complete current default-filter set, including Region and Campaign. The browser always applies this current list when restoring prepared Dashboards, and versioned selection and preview caches prevent older snapshots from hiding newly introduced default filters. Opening a workspace now compares its persistent Dashboard cache signature with the current application and cache-format versions, removes outdated chart, preview, projection and selection artifacts, and retains caches already written by the current versions. Large selections continue using their upload-time profile catalogues, but any missing filter catalogue is now read from the combined CDR tables in one grouped pass per CDR type, so older profiles expose the current fields without re-uploading their datasets. Its additional-filter picker includes every selected-CDR column and Auto-calculated Field, uses the same-width searchable in-menu picker as the filters, hides nonmatching fields as the user types, and immediately loads each added field's values in an independent request that respects the current datasets, dates and other filters without preparing the Dashboard. Each filter has a confirmed circular removal action that also hides default filters until restored. Save Filters persists only adaptive/additional and hidden-filter state, while Reload Saved Filters restores those filters without changing the current Scope, CDRs or dates. Navigating to another module with unsaved filter changes now opens a three-option warning to save, discard or cancel the navigation. Dashboard terminology, persistence and export format now use `dashboard` consistently, while existing stored configurations and export files migrate on use.
- Kept Dashboard chart text at a uniform scale while chart geometry fills its complete placeholder; charts without a bottom legend now extend vertical and distribution bars into the released lower area, and their larger column-aggregation captions reserve space from the actual horizontal or rotated labels instead of always keeping the maximum margin. The compact dataset, expanded-chart and zoom controls now appear together on hover or keyboard focus, then hide shortly after pointer exit; expanded charts open in a focused panel inset 5% from every side of the Dashboard viewer and retain dataset and zoom controls. Mouse-dragging a rectangle at 100% zooms to the selected chart area in both the Dashboard and single-chart expanded preview, while dragging a zoomed chart continues to pan it with an open-hand cursor that closes during the drag. Dashboard slides can also be navigated with the left and right arrow keys outside editable fields.
- Made Title Page dashboard titles substantially larger than their subtitles and coloured those subtitles yellow.
- Reduced the Dashboard chart-control hover delay to half a second and aligned CDF horizontal guides to 0%, 25%, 50%, 75% and 100%.
- Refined structural Dashboard slides with a wider, slightly smaller Title Page title, a less condensed yellow subtitle and more balanced Transition Slide title sizing.
- Reordered Dashboard slide navigation so the selector precedes compact First, Previous, Next and Last icon controls with accessible labels and hover tooltips.
- Reserved the Dashboard viewer subtitle line when it is empty so slide navigation stays in the same vertical position.
- Closing, applying or saving from the floating Adaptative Filters panel now returns directly to the chart without a confirmation because pending filters remain in the main Dashboard panel; its grey Close action appears only inside that floating panel. Filter controls awaiting Apply retain their amber highlight and show an amber `Unapplied filters` badge before `Unsaved filters`; once applied but not saved, their highlight and the remaining `Unsaved filters` badge become pastel red. View Dashboard and Generate PPT now intercept unapplied filter changes and offer Apply Filters and Continue, Discard and Continue, Save and Continue, or Cancel before continuing automatically with the requested action. Discard restores the last applied filter selection, Apply waits for its preparation to finish, and Save persists the filters before preparing them; pending changes limited to Scope, CDRs or dates prepare automatically without this dialog. Reload Saved Filters restores only persisted filters. The Date from and Date to textboxes contain small, discreet Use oldest and Use newest actions that retain the literal `Oldest` and `Newest` markers in the temporary universe, display their current resolved range as `Oldest (YYYY-MM-DD)` and `Newest (YYYY-MM-DD)`, and resolve them from the selected CDRs on every preparation, while calendar selections remain fixed dates for the current session. The PowerPoint filter-choice dialog keeps Cancel, Use Saved Filters and Use Current Filters together on one row. Applying a previously prepared combination restores its cached dataset and charts from memory or session storage, using a preparation key unaffected by Dashboard names, slide comments or the order in which equivalent filter values and datasets were selected.
- Made floating Adaptative Filter edits regenerate the visible Dashboard charts after a short debounce while retaining their unsaved, synchronized temporary state; each CDR source, scope, filter and date that differs from the last saved Dashboard state receives an amber highlight that clears on save, while merely opening a filter no longer triggers preparation; date controls are redrawn after their derived defaults become the saved baseline, preventing a stale amber state beside disabled filter actions. Applying a changed selection replaces any in-flight preparation so the resulting charts and row-count summary always match those filters; returning to the already applied state instead cancels temporary preparation, removes its progress notice and restores the existing Dashboard model and row-count summary without recalculation. Both preparation cards use the `Preparing Dashboard data` title. Multi-select filter menus now remain open while values are selected and close one second after the pointer leaves.
- Made Dashboard names editable directly in Manage Dashboards with a compact green save check inside the field that appears only while the name is being edited, and added a green eye action that opens the selected Dashboard straight in View Dashboard, including a centered loading card that closes automatically when its slides or updated charts are ready; the close action uses pastel red. Generate PPT from Manage Dashboards now asks whether its temporary universe should use Operator Comparison or Multivendor Comparison before queueing the job.
- Fixed application startup and Dashboard creation, duplication, deletion and reloads so they never wait for analytical warm-up jobs; the library displays its loading/operation state immediately, reads its persisted no-cache state, cancels obsolete preparation when opening the newly created Dashboard, and does not warn about changes when the management name, NR Mode and Template fields are being used to create it. Real unsaved filter changes continue to require confirmation.
- Added a bounded server-side Canvas-model warm-up queue for saved Dashboards. It uses each Dashboard’s persisted filters and template, writes only reusable JSON models (never PNGs), reports live progress in the global background-task card and retains a lightweight per-user snapshot after releasing its large temporary DataFrames. Canvas models and legacy PIL PNGs are stored under each workspace’s hidden `.dashboard-data-cache/charts-canvas` and `.dashboard-data-cache/charts-pil` directories, respectively. The Workspace management table provides a confirmed pastel-red clear-cache action that first cancels active or queued warming for that workspace, then removes these derived artifacts in a tracked background task without changing workspace data or settings and queues a fresh cache check only after removal completes. The action remains on the current page, immediately refreshes the floating-task card and refreshes both Size and Cache Size after completion. Users with access now follows Workspace; the Workspace column is twice its previous width and Users with access uses a compact width. Status remains immediately before Actions and uses distinct badges for active, ready, duplicating, cache-clearing, deleting, unavailable and failed workspaces; Active has stronger emphasis, and its Close workspace action replaces Open workspace before Duplicate workspace while background deletion keeps its row visible until completion. Completed background tasks remain visible for five seconds. OpenStreetMap map tiles use the hidden `data/.map-tiles-cache/openstreetmap` directory. Dashboard warm-up executes one preparation at a time; the background card distinguishes the active preparation from queued Dashboards and reports their queue position. Direct Dashboard data/filter preparation also appears in that card while its yellow notice is visible. Shared warm-up snapshots resolve for every permitted workspace user as soon as filtering completes: opening a Dashboard reuses its selection and available models immediately while the background worker fills missing models; model cache keys include the saved definition and selection state, so changed filters or datasets generate fresh models.
- Added `Dashboards` as an independent Admin export, import, transfer, backup and selective-restore component, positioned before Report Templates. Dashboard archives use versioned JSON definitions containing the name, NR mode, template, adaptive/additional and hidden filters, and comments, while deliberately excluding the temporary Scope/CDR/date universe and generated caches. Backup manifests now declare this separately archived component so Restore always offers it, Database Viewer gives Dashboard selection and PPT tables readable names, and Full Workspace and Full Environment archives include the component for every selected workspace. Including generated outputs now copies, archives and restores `output/dashboards` together with reports and chart sets, rebasing Dashboard PPT job paths for duplicated and imported workspaces; the duplicate, workspace export and Full Environment labels consistently refer to generated dashboards, reports and chart sets. Admin's Export / Transfer panel now uses Backup Content's grouped single-choice selector, including its section headings and separators, for Configuration Content, Workspace Content, Full Workspace and a final Full Environment section; its menu uses the full panel width, fits the current choices in common desktop viewports, wraps long option labels, overlays following content and scrolls internally instead of being clipped.
- Reordered E2E Reporting so Reports and Charts Jobs appears before the Charts Panel. Its table preserves natural row heights and dynamically fits the three most recent entries before using a vertical scrollbar; with three or fewer jobs it shrinks to its content, and the column header stays visible while scrolling through older report and Chart Set jobs. Completed Report and Chart Set jobs show their persisted elapsed time beside the final progress percentage, and retries reset the start timestamp so the duration covers only the latest execution. ID, Date, Template and Scope reserve enough space for their full values, with Date split cleanly across date and time lines and balanced against the slightly wider Scope column to avoid horizontal overflow. Datasets uses a compact yellow icon that exposes every grouped dataset in a viewport-safe tooltip and an Escape-dismissible floating panel while retaining the complete value for Excel-style column filtering. Renamed Technology to NR Mode in the NetCheck CDR Reports form, job filters and job table.
- Kept Report Templates database-backed through import, export, transfer, backup and restore flows. These operations no longer create workspace template directories, and remove the obsolete `slides-templates` directory when it is present.
- Restored profile-based filter catalogues for Dashboard selections while retaining exact filtered CDR counts and filtered chart-data previews through selection-scoped SQL queries. Full, unfiltered selections now obtain adaptive-filter values directly from the upload-time profiles, avoiding wide DISTINCT scans while keeping their materialized row selection and exact counts; active filters still calculate their dependent values from the filtered combined rows. Real UK workspace benchmarks retain the revision-keyed auxiliary projection: its 21-column Speech table took 12.839 seconds to build once and 0.804 seconds to read 562,430 rows, versus 15.490 seconds from the 285-column combined table. Projection construction now uses the measured safe SQLite configuration (32 KiB pages, WAL/NORMAL, memory-backed temporary data, a 256 MiB cache and 4 GiB memory mapping), improving the measured Speech build by 11.6–14.9%; it remains sequential because parallel Data, Voice and Speech construction increased contention. Complete dataset selections use a measured sequential scan, while partial selections retain the faster dataset index. Dashboard preparation repairs required columns that remain empty for an individual selected CDR even when that CDR already contributes other combined-table rows, then builds or restores narrow projections and pushes physical template filters into indexed SQLite queries; per-chart inputs remain in the cancelable chart-rendering queue so dataset preparation does not scan the CDR once per chart; this prevents a newly added dataset and its Campaign from reusing an apparently complete one-Campaign model, while Clear cache can stop writers promptly; the clear task now reports each phase, bounds its writer wait and never removes files while a writer remains active. Rendering then reuses companion-chart frames, maps repeated Campaign/status values once, computes CDF tail convergence with an event sweep instead of quadratic rescans, and only trims the horizontal tail after at least 80% of curves, rounded up, exceed 99% and converge; it also pre-aggregates eligible 100% stacked charts in SQL with weighted results. The measured 112,781-row Data status model now receives 34 aggregate rows and builds its identical Canvas payload in 0.014–0.017 seconds. Direct data preparation is registered in the floating task card as soon as it begins and remains visible until it becomes chart rendering; queued workers distinguish `Queued Dashboard data` from `Queued Dashboard Charts`, with queued work simply marked `Queued`. Each Dashboard group shows a prominent `Dashboard - “Dashboard name”` label once above concise titles, with a darker yellow, blue or red background matching its task panel and white text for contrast. Completed tasks remain in their original list position for five seconds. Dashboards execute and appear in FIFO queue order, with each Dashboard completed before the next; up to three independent chart models still render concurrently within that Dashboard. Foreground preparation verifies and requeues every other persisted Dashboard after giving the visible Dashboard priority, including warm-ups missing from the in-memory queue. Changing Scope selects its fresh recent-CDR universe and prepares independent Operator or Multivendor chart models. Previously prepared filter combinations are retained in session cache and replace an in-flight preparation immediately when selected again. Loading data uses amber and both Data queued and Charts queued use grey in Manage Dashboards. Ready combined datasets whose row counts match their ready sources now use a light-grey row; incomplete, pending and recalculating combined datasets remain amber.
- Clear cache now deletes its Dashboard artifacts as soon as it is confirmed, while cancelled writers are prevented from committing a projection or publishing a Canvas model afterwards.
- Completed background tasks now show their elapsed time during the five-second completion display, and Dashboard preparation and queue states consistently refer to the filtered `dataset`.
- Added background PowerPoint export for Ready Dashboards from Manage Dashboards. Generate PPT sits immediately before View Dashboard and each action uses the exact prepared Dashboard definition, including its applied Scope and filters even before they are saved; when applied filters differ from the persisted Dashboard, a three-way dialog generates from the current applied snapshot, uses the saved filters instead or cancels. Each job writes its presentation plus persistent PNG, tooltip and interactive Canvas-model chart assets under `output/dashboards`, appears in the global background-task card, and remains available in the new `PowerPoint Generation Jobs` panel with progress, elapsed time, PPT and chart downloads, chart preview, stop, retry and delete actions. A Reporting-style Charts Panel at the end of E2E Dashboards identifies completed PPT Jobs through a readable high-contrast selector formatted as `local date · time  •  Dashboard Name  •  Scope`, emphasizes the Dashboard name, filters them by NR Mode, Dashboard, Template and Scope, initially opens the newest matching job, displays its cached chart gallery with uniformly sized images that fill their cards and opens each thumbnail in the same expanded Canvas viewer used by a double-click in the live Dashboard, including zoom, refresh and chart navigation without rerendering it. Historical previews hide Adaptative Filters, keep Auto-Calculated Fields and the job's exact Template editor available above the chart preview, and open Filtered Chart Dataset above that viewer. New jobs retain the exact Dashboard preview fingerprint and reuse its live or persisted materialized selection for fast chart-data access. Filtered Chart Dataset reads exact 100-row pages directly from the cached auxiliary projection whenever its filters are SQL-compatible, deduplicates source-row identities in fallback processing and never reports more rows than the selected CDR universe; every column provides an Excel-style value filter executed by the server across the complete chart dataset, with its value catalogue retained while other column filters are applied, filtered columns marked in pastel pink, filters retained during pagination and CSV download, a lower-left count showing the filtered rows against the chart total, a `Clear N filters` action and a lower-right Close action. The Charts Panel polling now detects newly completed PPT jobs, automatically selects the most recent matching job and refreshes its cached charts without a page reload, while serialized polling prevents an older response from restoring the previous job. The ready Dashboard card now distinguishes the selected `Dataset Universe` row totals from the active `Filtered Universe` totals with readable colour labels. Its chart canvases, including the preview and expanded viewers, use their entire template frame, and bar charts with a side legend now use the available lower plot area rather than reserving it for a nonexistent bottom legend; the Filtered Dataset action matches the refresh action's size, and their shared reset-zoom action uses a magnifying-glass `1:1` SVG. The row summary explicitly identifies chart-filtered rows because Template-level chart filters can reduce them below the Dashboard's per-CDR totals. Its Dashboard, Date and Scope header badges use distinct purple, blue and green backgrounds; a larger yellow View Filters action matches the PowerPoint Job selector height, aligns at its right and exposes the selected job's complete filter snapshot through the same viewport-safe tooltip and floating panel as the jobs table. Each exported slide also writes its saved Dashboard comments into the template commentary placeholder and renders every Canvas directly at its chart placeholder's exact proportion, allowing chart geometry to fill its full width and height while preserving uniformly scaled typography and complete titles, axes and legends without padding, cropping or deformation. The jobs table now shows Scope instead of Template, a compact ID, a two-line local Date/time and wider Dashboard column; its Open Dashboard charts action loads the corresponding job in the Charts Panel. Each PPT and its containing folder start with `yyyymmdd_HHMMSS  - Dashboard Name`. Dashboard definition exports now use the versioned ZIP structure accepted by Admin Import. PPT generation is also available from the Dashboard viewer, and E2E Dashboards restores the page scroll position after a reload; Slides and Charts columns are sized to keep their headings on one line. The PPT jobs table also retains the selected CDRs, date range and adaptive filters used at export time and exposes them through a Filters icon tooltip; its CDRs are grouped as Data, Voice and Speech like Reports, a yellow Filters icon shows all filters in a single compact viewport-safe hover tooltip and opens them in a readable floating panel, which can be closed with Escape, and it records the Dashboard NR Mode beside the generation date. Its separated red Delete All PPTs action now includes a clear trash SVG. The left filter column groups CDR and date controls inside a `Dataset Universe` card headed `Select Dataset Universe`, with bold, vertically separated labels, and places Scope in its own `Dashboard Scope` card immediately above it. Both Dashboard PPT and Reporting job tables now support Excel-style multi-select filters from each data column header, with consistently styled solid-purple sticky headers, matching typography, fully visible fixed-size controls, solid header buttons and balanced column widths; headers may wrap across two lines only between words, and Report dataset names remain complete on separate lines. Reports and Charts Jobs now relies exclusively on those header filters; its red bulk deletion actions sit below the table, matching the separated Delete All PPTs action.
- Expanded every Dashboard Canvas chart type into its available preview area: bars and category spacing now scale with the data cardinality, while CDF, status, failure, distribution, scatter, map and table plots reserve space only for their actual axes and a visible legend. The shared geometry reserves a non-overlapping lane exclusively on the legend's configured top, bottom, left or right side, and uses that space for the plot when the chart has no legend. Dashboard Charts Panel cards remove inherited image padding and repaint persisted Canvas models with the current renderer, so existing job thumbnails receive corrected geometry while legacy jobs without a model retain their cached PNG fallback.
- Kept Manage Dashboards and every Dashboard PPT action unavailable while the selected dataset or its Canvas charts are preparing, queued or rendering; server-side export requests now enforce the same readiness rule. Dashboard Datasets & Filters places icon-labelled `Generate PPT` after `View Dashboard`, while the viewer places its PPT icon after Presentation mode; ready-card summaries restore exact selected `Dataset Universe` totals for earlier cached previews, with `Filtered Universe` using the `View Filters` yellow. The Charts Panel's yellow `View Filters` action retains its tooltip and floating snapshot panel, now with a funnel SVG and more legible text.
- Added the live Dashboard-preparation percentage bar to both yellow in-page notices: below Dashboard Datasets & Filters and centred in View Dashboard. Both poll the active foreground task without duplicating its work, retain their explanatory text, and show the current source, universe, projection or chart-rendering phase with a visible percentage below the bar.
- Kept View Dashboard available in the floating Adaptative Filters panel. It closes that panel after resolving any changed filters or Dataset Universe preparation, returning focus to the updated Dashboard viewer below it.
- Dashboard preview caches now restore across server restarts and are shared by all users of the workspace. The cache lookup receives the temporary Scope, CDR and date universe as well as saved filters, verifies the saved selection against current CDR revisions, and retains up to 128 recent filtered selections instead of only eight; unchanged source-column validation is also reused without rescanning the complete selected CDRs.
- Styled the Default Filters panel and its dropdown triggers, menus, search, actions and options in a consistent pastel-blue palette to distinguish its adaptive dataset filters from Dataset Universe controls. The right-hand grid keeps its title and Default Filters at content height so they align exactly with Dashboard Scope, while Additional Filters expands to finish level with Dataset Universe.
- Dashboard preparation now returns its ready Dataset Universe and filters before asynchronously rebuilding unrelated Dashboard warm-up entries, so the green ready state and View Dashboard/Generate PPT actions appear without waiting for that maintenance work. Opening E2E Dashboards no longer waits for library warm-ups or status/PPT polling: its persistent-cache lookup immediately shows the yellow preparation card and global task, then promotes the result to ready when restored or prepared. Persistent Dataset Universe date bounds are revision-keyed and reused across server restarts, while combined CDR tables now index dataset/date pairs, so cache validation avoids repeated full date scans. Refresh is visible and enabled only for changed Dashboard input; the green ready-card check and orange out-of-sync indicator are mutually exclusive, with the latter shown only for changed Scope, CDR or date universe controls. Reloading retains the user’s original scroll position instead of jumping to Charts Panel after preparation. Manage Workspaces refreshes Cache Size while open after projections, manifests or Canvas models are written or pruned.

#### 🐛 Bug fixes:
- Fixed CDF previews discarding valid samples when optional Campaign metadata is empty, and kept every percentage label on the left axis fully inside the Canvas at all preview and expanded-view sizes.
- Fixed recurring Report Template backups for workspaces whose database had not yet been initialized, and prevented obsolete compatibility CSV directories from being rescanned after their one-time database migration.
- Fixed Dashboard creation, duplication and import in browsers that do not implement `crypto.randomUUID`.
- Fixed E2E Dashboards adaptive-filter preparation by exposing every requested dimension immediately, displaying a loading state instead of empty facet values while selected CDRs are prepared, loading custom-field values when the field is added, reducing large CDR projections to the columns required for filtering and rendering, and enabling View Dashboard only after the combined data and dashboard definition are ready. Profile catalogues resolve fields through their canonical profile key, and combined-table fallbacks ignore the facet's own saved restriction, so the Operator selector exposes every value available under the other active filters instead of only its previously selected values. The Dataset Universe summary now reuses the already calculated effective row counts when no date or adaptive-filter restriction is active, so it matches Filtered Universe without launching separate full-table counting scans; restricted selections retain their inexpensive upload-profile source totals and make an active partial selection visible instead of presenting it as All values. Date fields now default to the earliest and latest values available across the selected CDRs and use a bounded calendar; navigating its months never changes the displayed date or refreshes the Dashboard, while selecting a day does. Bounds appear with CDR selection, NR Mode and Scope, while the redundant template filter has been removed. Non-applicable Auto-calculated Fields no longer advance combined-table revisions on every opening and invalidate otherwise reusable Dashboard data.
- Reserved a right-side lane for Dashboard failure-chart legends whenever the resolved legend contains items, preventing them from overflowing the chart canvas.
- Fixed the shared Canvas chart renderer to prefer a working Chrome binary over Ubuntu's unavailable Chromium Snap wrapper in automated test environments. Dashboard creation, duplication and import now adopt the persisted canonical definition immediately, and Dashboard dirty-state comparison normalizes defaults and key order, preventing false unsaved-change warnings and stale client library state after reload.
- Fixed a concurrent legacy-dataset materialization race that could recreate a SQLite dataset table between replacement steps and interrupt Dataset Analysis loading.
- Kept authenticated sessions scoped to the running application process, so restarting the desktop application or Docker container requires a new login instead of restoring a previous session from the database.
- Fixed expanded Dashboard charts to reuse the already-rendered chart model, hide their loading layer after painting the enlarged canvas and report non-JSON chart-service responses clearly.
- Fixed Dashboard chart legends so stacked status segments, distribution buckets, scatter series and map points always use the exact colours shown in their plotted data; legacy slash-separated captions remain available only when they cover every plotted series.
- Fixed hierarchical column-bar charts so first-level aggregation boundaries are continuous from their header through the plot, while lower-level boundaries remain lighter; mean-bar values now sit inside sufficiently tall bars for clearer comparison.
- Fixed multivendor chart hierarchies across Dashboard, Report and Chart Set renderers: each operator is now a first-level aggregation with its vendors nested beneath it in configured order, without generating invalid vendor/operator combinations. CDF legends and tooltips retain the complete Operator · Vendor · Campaign identity of every curve. In bar charts, first-level boundaries are continuous from their header through the plot, while nested vendor and campaign boundaries are dashed and begin beneath their parent header; third-level captions have dedicated spacing. Hierarchical labels remain horizontal when they fit, rotate 45° only when needed and 90° only if the diagonal label still overflows. Nested row boundaries begin after their parent label column, and cached Dashboard chart models are regenerated with this structure.
- Fixed the generic NSA `Tableau Charts` and `Tableau Dashboards` templates so Rows and Column Aggregation omit `G_Level_1` and use `Operator`, while filters and legends use `Vendor` rather than `Vendor_V3`; Vendor Comparison expands the generic operator level at render time without changing the stored templates.
- Fixed bar-value labels across Dashboard, Report and Chart Set Canvas and PIL renderers: labels remain horizontal when they fit inside a bar, rotate 90° when vertical placement fits better, and otherwise remain visible outside the bar. Percentages for thin stacked segments are now explicitly left-aligned beyond the bar and use a compact white backing, keeping red failure labels legible without overlapping their red segment.
- Expanded `Vendor` filters to accept either full `Operator_Vendor` values or bare vendor names; bare `IN`, `NOT IN`, equality and contains filters now match that vendor across every operator.
- Standardized semantic outcome colours across charts: Success, Completed and equivalent outcomes use stable distinct green shades, while Failure, Failed, Dropped, Cutoff, Error and equivalent outcomes use stable distinct red shades. 100% status bars now also classify generic `Success` and `Failure` labels semantically; Dashboard models regenerate with the corrected palette.
- Renamed the E2E Dashboard viewer position label from `Dashboard` to `Slide`, matching the template catalogue terminology.
- Standardized chart operator order across Dashboard, Report and Chart Set renderers: `VF`, `3`, `EE`, `O2`, then all remaining operators.
- Made the server the sole owner of Dashboard chart prefetching and persistent models. Completed preparations now persist a lightweight preview manifest beside the Canvas models, allowing application restarts and every permitted workspace user to restore slides, filters and selection immediately without rebuilding them. A Dashboard opened during preparation reuses its available models while the same server worker finishes the rest; the browser requests only charts on the visible slide and no longer launches a second `Rendering Dashboard charts` cache pass. It also waits for the existing server preparation instead of starting a duplicate one. Every Apply Filters action now checks the complete Dashboard model cache, queues every missing chart with live floating-task progress and keeps prior filter-combination models available, so restoring previously cached filters avoids recalculation. Automatic Dashboard initialization no longer causes a false unsaved-changes prompt when opening another Dashboard.
- Added live server Dashboard-rendering progress to the floating background-task card, using `Rendering Dashboard Charts` for active work and `Queued Dashboard Charts` while it waits for the serial renderer. Manage Dashboards now shows a compact, colour-coded Status badge for ready, data-loading, queued, rendering, missing-chart and failed states, refreshed live without rebuilding its table rows. Expanded chart view now includes Adaptative Filters, Auto-Calculated Fields and template editing controls; template editing focuses the exact row for the expanded chart. Its `Chart n / total` pre-title sits above the title, navigation is placed below the canvas, and Refresh, Dataset and Zoom controls appear together inside the canvas only while it is hovered or focused. Rectangular zoom only activates over the plot area, never over a chart title or the floating controls; available floating actions receive pointer events and show a pointer cursor, and Refresh uses a clear conventional circular-arrow icon. Clicking the backdrop closes the expanded chart. A single global Dashboard data gate now prevents foreground and automatic dataset-preparation phases from processing in parallel. Applying filters interrupts every automatic warm-up, waits only for an active dataset scan to release the gate, prepares the visible Dashboard first and then requeues the interrupted Dashboards; cancelled chart-rendering batches no longer retain the gate and background tasks remain grouped into stable per-Dashboard sections even when their server order changes. Every queued or running Dashboard preparation and chart warm-up now exposes the shared Interrupt task action and uses cooperative cancellation without leaving the Dashboard in an error state. Floating Dashboard Filters, Template Editor, Auto-calculated Fields, Dataset and Presentation panels also close on a pointer press outside their dialog; Filters, Template Editor and Auto-calculated Fields ask to keep editing when they contain unsaved changes. Closing an unchanged Template Editor keeps the existing prepared Dashboard data, while a saved template edit refreshes it.
- Enabled double-clicking an available E2E Dashboard chart to open the same expanded-chart viewer as the Expand Chart control.
- Added first, previous, next and last navigation controls to the expanded E2E Dashboard chart viewer, traversing all available charts in dashboard order.
- Fixed Canvas dashboard rendering becoming stuck on high-cardinality Multi KPI CDF charts, including `Tableau Charts` canvas 68. The renderer no longer creates a DataFrame copy for each curve and collapses only excessive leading identifier levels before producing a bounded, readable set of comparison curves.
- Fixed E2E Dashboard initialization failing with a JavaScript syntax error in the ready-dataset notice, which left Manage Dashboards permanently showing `Loading Dashboards…`. Expired API sessions now return a JSON sign-in error instead of redirecting Dashboard PowerPoint generation to an HTML login page, and the Dashboard client reports any other non-JSON response clearly.
- Fixed Dashboard reopening so a queued automatic warm-up is checked once and then yields immediately to the visible Dashboard's foreground preparation instead of polling its serial queue for up to two minutes. Charts whose required CDR type is absent from the selected Dataset Universe now remain empty with a clear missing-dataset message.

#### 📚 Documentation:
- Reorganized and expanded the English documentation around E2E Dashboards as the primary workflow: its guide now covers management, dataset universes, alias-aware filters and decisions, cache reuse, viewer controls, filtered chart data, comments, presentation, PowerPoint jobs and maintenance, while E2E Reporting retains its classic Report and Chart Set workflow. Centralized the full Report Template schema, structural and multi-chart slides, supported chart types, worked recipes, filter language, aggregations, legends, layouts and colours under Administration, with direct cross-document links that now preserve and scroll to heading anchors in the in-app Help viewer. Updated Readme, Product Overview, Technical Considerations, Configuration, Help index, Project Structure and Administration to reflect database-backed templates; role safeguards; template lifecycle/editor behaviour; global, registry and workspace database ownership; current Dashboard selection, projection, preview and Canvas cache layers; distinct Dashboard PPT jobs and output; granular backup/restore and portability boundaries; Database Viewer and Datasets Management actions; Node/Chromium rendering; bind-address and Docker port behaviour; complete runtime/storage variables; and colour-coded floating cards for active-workspace, other-workspace and system background tasks. Help Home now opens with the same centred Dashboard Analytic logo as Readme. Also renamed Datasets Analysis documentation, renumbered subsequent Help chapters and documented the canonical `/e2e-reporting` route and legacy redirect.
- Added a release navigation panel and collapsible Release sections to the Changelog document view, generated from its Release headings.

---

## Release: v0.2.3
### Release Date: 2026-09-11
#### ⚠️ Breaking Changes:
- Workspace duplication no longer includes generated Reports and Chart Sets by default. Select the new inclusion option when a duplicate must retain the source `output` tree; imported workspaces continue to rebase copied report artifacts so Reporting can show their chart thumbnails.
- Renamed the workspace table `calculated_dimensions` to `autocalculated_fields`. Existing workspace databases migrate automatically, but direct SQL queries, scripts and integrations must use the new table name.
- Renamed Slides Templates to Report Templates and moved their CSV content into each workspace's `report_templates` table. Existing CSV templates migrate automatically when their workspace is opened and their compatibility directories are then removed; integrations reading template files or using the former terminology must be updated.
- Unified Backup and Import / Export ZIP layouts under `workspaces/<workspace name>/`, removed the intermediate `full-workspace` directory, store Report Template CSV files in `report-templates/` and Auto-calculated Fields as `auto-calculated-fields/auto-calculated-fields.json`. External ZIP readers and writers must adopt the new paths.
- Replaced the flat archive manifest component list across Backup, Restore, Import / Export and Transfer. Packages now declare only `app_database` and/or `workspace_components` in `components`, with `workspace_components` separately listing `workspace_database`, `input`, `output`, `report_templates` and/or `auto_calculated_fields`; backup settings, automation and external manifest consumers must adopt this schema.

#### 🌟 New Features:
- Added global floating background-task panels on every authenticated page: active-workspace and server tasks appear at the lower right, while tasks belonging to other workspaces appear in a differently coloured lower-left panel headed by the workspace name; concurrent groups remain visible together with live progress until all work finishes.
- Added a persistent minimize/expand control to every floating background-task card.
- Added cached OpenStreetMap road basemaps to PNG Map charts only. The renderer retrieves missing tiles on first use, stores them under `data/map-tiles-cache/openstreetmap`, reuses them for later charts and falls back to the existing coordinate grid if tiles are unavailable; all other chart types remain offline and unchanged.

#### 🚀 Enhancements:
- Added hover help to the interactive-tooltips option in Report and Chart Set generation confirmations, explaining that it enables chart-value context on pointer hover and adds a small generation-time cost.
- Added a blue Open workspace action with the 📂 folder icon immediately after Save in Manage Workspaces, and clarified the Save tooltip as `Save workspace changes`.
- Workspace duplication completion now updates its row in place without reloading the page, preserving the open state of Manage Workspaces.
- Workspace deletion now runs in the background, appears in the lower-left task panel and removes only its table row on completion without reloading or collapsing Manage Workspaces.
- Split CDR ingestion from combined-table regeneration: a dataset now reaches `Ready` at 100% and refreshes the Datasets table before its combined CDR is rebuilt as a separately tracked background task with its own progress in the global task panel.
- Updated the Readme, Changelog and Help navigation badges to use clear page-specific labels and stronger contrast, and added matching active-workspace totals for Reports, Chart Sets and all Jobs to the E2E Reporting header and Reports and Charts Jobs cards.
- Increased the contrast of Collapse and Expand badges on white panels with a neutral grey background, border and text.
- Added confirmed `Stop Job` controls to the global background-task cards for accessible-workspace duplication, dataset processing, Report and Chart Set generation, Auto-calculated Field materialization, combined CDR recreation and single-workspace exports/imports. Stopped duplications clean partial files and registry/access rows, stopped materializations retain a red status, stopped exports remove temporary files, and an import cannot be stopped once it has started.
- Replaced the stop glyph with a centered 0.56rem white square inside the circular `Stop Job` control, preserving the button dimensions and making its size consistent across browsers.
- Moved bulk deletion of all Reports and all Chart Sets into workspace background tasks with live progress in the global task panels; Reporting remains available and refreshes its job tables when each deletion completes.
- Kept the Open and Remove workspace actions disabled for the active workspace after its row is refreshed following background duplication.
- Added a Database Backups subpanel in Admin > Database Management with visually consistent **On demand backup** and **Scheduled backups** sections. On demand backup has side-by-side **Backup** and **Restore** panels: Backup creates a tracked ZIP from clearly labelled Configuration Content or Workspace Content. Workspace Content offers individual Workspace Database, Report Templates (one CSV per template), Auto-calculated Fields (one JSON definition file), Input raw datasets and Output reports/Chart Sets selections; Application database plus the first three workspace components are selected by default, while Input and Output are opt-in. The accessible-workspaces multi-select appears whenever workspace content is selected and no separate full-workspace selector is needed. Each ZIP records its selected app and workspace components in a versioned manifest; Restore uses it, with a structural fallback for older ZIPs, to detect available granular restore parts in its overwrite confirmation. Restore lists ZIPs in the chosen server-visible path, detects their available components and affected workspace names, and requires a structured overwrite warning confirmation with individually selectable restore parts and a grey Cancel action. Each panel validates only its own inputs, so Backup Now never requires a Restore file. The Restore file list refreshes after immediate backups and periodically while Admin is open, covering scheduled backups too. Manual backup cards expose **Stop Job** to their owner; stopping removes the incomplete ZIP. Both operations appear in the floating task card and neither they nor saving scheduler settings reloads or moves the Admin page; **Save scheduler settings** posts to the scheduler endpoint correctly. Backup notices stay in Backup protection, including compatibility redirects from prior URLs; **Refresh table** now uses the same burgundy action colour as **Backup Now**. Backups skip stale workspace-registry entries without a workspace database and use each workspace's visible name rather than its internal ID for the archive tree, preventing phantom or misleading workspace folders. Its high-contrast hover state preserves the selected-content label. Default storage is `config/scheduled-backups`; a large container-visible directory browser can create folders without entering them and is restricted to the application `config` directory, with fixed professional Home and Up controls, uniform high-contrast folder rows and an independently scrolling directory list; the wide, styled multi-select picker keeps each content option on one line with left-aligned checkboxes; retention defaults to 30 backups; and recurrence shows only the applicable weekday/month-day field before execution time and retention.
- Reordered Manage Workspaces actions so Duplicate precedes Open, expanded the New workspace and Open workspace fields to 800px in desktop layouts with their actions kept alongside, and remove cancelled workspace duplications from the live table once their partial copy is cleaned up.
- Moved Database View Actions to the first sticky column so row actions remain visible while horizontally scrolling, and styled the row Save action in green.
- Scheduled database backups now appear live in Server Tasks with phase-aware progress and refresh Backup Protection status without reloading Admin. Backup filenames, Restore lists and Last successful backup consistently use the ZIP creation time; backup storage and its constrained server-side picker now default to `data/scheduled-backups`; and changing Backup Content, Workspaces to include, Backup folder or Enable recurring backups persists immediately, while Save scheduler settings persists recurrence, day, execution time and retention.
- Kept Server Tasks in the right floating-task dock, styled active-workspace task cards green and non-active workspace/server cards soft pastel yellow, and exposed the global `application_state` table as **Application State** in Database Viewer.
- Increased cached OpenStreetMap Map-chart source resolution to street-level zoom, with a bounded 48-tile maximum per cold render, so roads and labels remain sharper in generated PNGs.

#### 🐛 Bug fixes:
- Fixed the Reporting Chart Preview header so Auto-calculated Fields appears beside its red Close control; Escape now leaves active Auto-calculated Fields and filtered-dataset child dialogs open, closing the preview only when no child dialog is active.
- Matched the Auto-calculated Fields dialog's circular close control to the Chart Preview close button and styled its text Close control in red with white text; each field deletion control uses a thick white minus on muted burgundy.
- Made Slides Templates Editor chart image and data previews load the chosen CDR type from its materialized combined reporting table instead of concatenating every individual CDR table.
- Fixed Slides Templates Editor Chart Preview so every template filter value is displayed and retained in its Filter builder.
- Added a non-applying Close action beside the red Update Template button in Slides Templates Editor Chart Preview; updating now requires confirmation that the displayed chart's template row will receive the Interactive Preview values.
- Fixed Auto-calculated Fields managers so deleted and duplicated fields update the displayed list immediately, before background materialization; their compact dialog keeps its header, close control and bottom actions fixed while only long field-card lists scroll.
- Added compact-screen pagination to the Workspace Auto-calculated Fields definitions list.
- Fixed Report Templates inspection in Database Viewer by rendering stored CSV BLOB values as text, capitalized `Workspace State` in the table selector, stopped completed incoming-transfer receipts from lingering as active Server Tasks, and restored legible Select all/Select none controls in the incoming transfer workspace picker.
- Fixed immediate Report Template editing after an in-place rename by returning the canonical renamed identifier and updating the row’s editor action without requiring an Admin page reload.
- Fixed OpenStreetMap Map-chart tile downloads on Python installations whose system certificate chain is incomplete by using the installed CA bundle while retaining TLS certificate validation.

#### 📚 Documentation:
- Documented Database Management's **Backup Protection** and **Database Viewer** subsections, including granular application/workspace backup content, accessible workspace selection, on-demand and scheduled ZIPs, Restore content detection with Configuration/Workspace grouping and overwrite confirmation, config-scoped folder and ZIP pickers, retention, Docker storage requirements, combined CDR tables and floating-task progress with confirmed Stop Job controls.

---

## Release: v0.2.2
### Release Date: 2026-09-07
#### ⚠️ Breaking Changes:
- Moved status-row inclusion out of the chart renderer and into explicit template filters such as `Test_Result IN (Completed, Dropped, Failed)`; update affected templates so their status-chart denominator remains visible and editable.
- Slides Templates now belong to individual workspaces, including the template registry and CSV files; Database Management exposes the registry as Slides Templates registry under Workspace Tables. A one-time migration corrects legacy workspace paths that still point into the project tree, copies the former shared library to every workspace that already existed, registers each copy in its own database, removes the obsolete global registry and legacy configuration library only after success, while newly created workspaces, including Default, start without templates.

#### 🌟 New Features:
- Added an Edit Slides Template action to the active Charts Panel, opening the exact generating template in a full editor modal without the template selector.
- Added manual Refresh and silent five-second polling to App Logs, preserving active filters and the Executed by selector's state and options; filters are faceted from every loaded row matching the other active filters, including compact-phone cards.
- Added a Cancel operation action to outgoing server-transfer progress, cooperatively stopping connection, approval waiting, export or transmission and cleaning temporary output.
- Added the NSA `Tableau Charts` Slides Template with one slide for each of the workbook's 86 individual chart worksheets, excluding dashboards and auxiliary sheets, plus `Tableau Dashboards` with the workbook's 20 dashboards and every chart arranged on its corresponding slide; preserved Tableau titles, filters, legends and row/column aggregations, translated categorical ratios, percentiles and auto-calculated fields such as vendor split, result groups, bandwidths, throughput buckets, first LTE ARFCN, TTFP ratio and historical eMOCN/NNS, and added native latitude/longitude Map and multi-KPI CDF chart types.

#### 🚀 Enhancements:
- Made incoming transfer approvals reliable across every super-admin page, expired stale pending offers after 15 minutes, reused equivalent retries, propagated terminal source cancellation to the destination, and respected standard HTTP/HTTPS ports when a complete destination URL is entered without an explicit port.
- Preserved every explicitly filtered `IN` category in hierarchical chart grids even when it has zero matching samples; rendered status bars without unpainted `Cutoff` remainders with Completed in green and Cutoff/Dropped/Failed in red shades; moved the Tableau templates' `100% Stacked Vertical Bars` dimensions to Columns; and rendered every aggregation as a visible nested level, with all Rows levels on the left, every Columns level except the last above the plot, only the last below it, and no repeated compound captions.
- Increased and strengthened chart titles, axis values and bar-value labels across the shared renderers, and rebuilt legends with larger bold captions, markers, line samples and spacing for reliable preview and report scaling.
- Normalised App Logs users to lowercase with case-insensitive filtering, recorded explicit successful/failed login outcomes, and limited UI auditing to meaningful operations with descriptive actions such as report or Chart Set generation.
- Automatically opened and scrolled to the Charts Panel when a newly queued or retried Report Job completes successfully, matching the existing Charts Job workflow.
- Preselected the two most recently uploaded processed CDRs of each Data, Voice and Speech type in E2E Reporting, falling back to the sole available dataset when only one exists, and restored two recent selections on return from Vendor Comparison when necessary.
- Reduced each CDR selector to its most recently uploaded selected dataset when switching to Vendor Comparison, without replacing an existing single selection.
- Clarified Chart Set audit stages as generation requested, rendering completed and published; App Logs retains the initiating user in `User` and identifies background execution as `system` in the new `Executed by` column.
- Accelerated repeated multi-platform Docker builds with a stable shared BuildKit cache and isolated Python dependencies from font/timezone package changes, preventing unnecessary slow ARM64 `pip` reinstalls.
- Unified the inactive Workspace, E2E Dashboard, E2E Reporting and Chart Builder tabs with the Chart Builder palette while preserving each module's existing active colour.
- Ordered Campaign aggregation values chronologically from the oldest to the newest across every shared chart renderer, independently of dataset ingestion order.
- Set the default server-transfer destination port to `7278` while respecting an explicit URL port; the dialog remembers the last destination and submits from either field with Enter.
- Standardised Vendor Comparison charts by automatically excluding Mixed and Other vendors, resolving template Operator filters from the materialised operator prefix, preserving full vendor identities in aggregations and legends, and ordering mapped vendors consistently while retaining chronological Campaign values; where Vendor is the primary aggregation, Ericsson uses green shades, Huawei red, NSN blue, Samsung purple, Mixed and Other yellow, and blank values grey.
- Reworked Slides Templates Editor as a shared 90%-viewport dialog opened from Admin template actions or the active Charts Panel, with its selected name in the title, footer and corner Close actions, a fixed action bar and table-only scrolling; the embedded editor uses a lightweight route and clear yellow change markers that reset after save.
- Invalidated the active workspace-size snapshot after dataset, report and Chart Set files are created, completed, retried or removed.
- Fitted CDF legends into six evenly spaced entries per row when positioned above or below the chart, preserved each series' colour and line thickness with clearer latest-campaign samples, and sized left/right legend lanes to avoid wasted space or axis overlap.
- Rendered Distribution chart bucket legends as the ordered, colour-matched bucket ranges used by the stacked bars instead of displaying the raw `Buckets` filter expression.
- Rendered `Threshold` legends as two colour-matched ranges derived from the configured value (`< threshold` and `≥ threshold`), matching the red and green stacked-chart segments.
- Moved incoming transfer offers from hidden package-directory JSON files into the global SQLite database shared by Docker workers, automatically migrating legacy files; per-source replacement is atomic, new requests supersede older unreviewed offers immediately, and startup self-repairs the offer table with actionable diagnostics.
- Made Database Management discover and group every global configuration and active-workspace table, exposing Application state, Server transfer offers, Users and the Slides Templates registry alongside workspace tables including `report_chart_jobs` as Chart Set jobs and `report_runs` as Generated Reports jobs.
- Accelerated every Interactive Preview by caching combined CDR frames and filter results separately, so presentation and aggregation edits no longer reload or refilter unchanged datasets; Chart Preview also retains each loaded interactive definition and prefetches adjacent charts during navigation, while superseded browser render requests are cancelled and ignored.
- Unified Reports Jobs and Charts Jobs in one chronologically ordered panel and one physical `generated_jobs` workspace table, automatically migrating and removing the two legacy job tables; the panel includes explicit Tech and job Type columns, concise shared job metadata, a compact Progress column, each job type's original actions wrapped within their available cell, a combined count and both bulk-delete controls.
- Kept workspace sizes synchronized across the active-workspace header badge, header switcher and Workspace Management controls after background or AJAX file changes, and made the username badge an explicit hoverable keyboard-accessible change-password button.
- Made Readme the default selected tab after signing in or opening the authenticated application root.
- Renamed the Workspace queue heading to Datasets and replaced dataset, user, template and database row actions with compact circular icon controls carrying accessible hover labels and clearer download symbols, preserving those icon controls throughout live queued, processing, ready, failed and stopped transitions.
- Consolidated the 390×844 compact-phone layout across every module with a single-line login title and compact three-card feature summary, tighter panels, forms, non-overflowing protected-role labels and Import / Export / Transfer controls; responsive cards for Users, Slides Templates, Reports and Charts Jobs, Datasets, Workspaces, transfers, App Logs and dynamic Database Management rows; labels above values, up to three short fields per row, paired dates, full-width IDs and left-aligned action rows; reliably visible Manage Workspaces content; and viewport-fitted chart, data and Slides Template editors with narrow action and slide columns, compact fixed controls, vertically stacked row actions, table-only scrolling and iOS-safe editable font sizes that prevent focus zoom. Compact Users cards place Active, Created and Actions on the same row, followed by a full-width Workspaces field as the final row, while Report Templates Library places Default and Actions on the same row. Dataset Analysis also hides the redundant default/empty filter-selection guidance on compact screens. Documentation adds a viewport-fixed collapsible right-side navigator matching the shared Modules and Sections controls across Help, Readme and Changelog; all three pages also retain an integrated Navigation panel on large screens, while compact layouts show only the fixed copy, and every Navigation list starts with Readme and Changelog before the separated Help index. The redundant Readme and Changelog tabs are omitted from the top header at every viewport size while Help remains visible. Navigation is vertically centred whenever it is the only right-side tab. Changelog likewise adds a separate fixed Releases tab on the right and places its integrated Releases list below integrated Navigation on large screens; compact Changelog hides both integrated copies, while the two right-side tabs align vertically with Modules and Sections and share their documentation palette.
- Paginated compact-phone card tables and Chart Set thumbnails to one visible item per page with compact icon-based First, Previous, Next and Last controls, thicker previous/next arrows, a centred page indicator above the controls, and automatic updates after live table refreshes, active filters and generated chart updates; filtering opens the first matching card, while App Logs preserves the selected card during manual or automatic refreshes and explicitly hides non-matches before pagination.
- Made workspace duplication preserve source access membership, defer reporting-row synchronization until required, complete in a background worker, clean interrupted partial copies through authorised workspace-admin actions, and redirect accidental GET requests to the POST-only operation.
- Reworked Chart Preview with a taller desktop dialog and a viewport-fitted mobile-landscape canvas: smaller, thicker chart navigation and header actions preserve the portrait layout; magnifying-glass zoom controls and boundary-aware overlaid pan arrows move the viewport in their labelled direction; report and Chart Set confirmation dialogs enable precomputed semantic tooltips by default but can omit them entirely, and enabled sidecars are versioned and written directly beside their PNGs in the final generation so obsolete hit-area geometry is regenerated on first use, while CDF hit testing is capped at 120 evenly spaced points per series and uses one reusable background worker for faster, smaller jobs; stale legacy staging directories are cleaned and Interactive Preview inspects only the selected datasets' schemas and explicitly identifies empty Rows, Columns and Filters selections; its new `Edit Template` action resolves the generating row through the editor's slide ordering and opens the originating Slides Template directly on that exact chart; targets use the renderer's exact CDF geometry and series identity to identify the line under the pointer, cover horizontal as well as vertical stacked-bar segments with their labels, values and legends after chart changes, zooming or panning; and the canvas repaints after mobile rotation while preserving zoom.
- Added native hover labels and accessible names to compact table action icons, strengthened the download arrows in Reports and Charts Jobs, and represented report downloads with a PPT presentation icon and explicit `Download PPT Report` tooltip beneath the green download arrow.
- Refined Workspace, Dataset and Slides Templates management tables with compact labelled actions, clearer Dashboard and chart glyphs, balanced metadata columns, readable date/time lines and flexible names without horizontal overflow.
- Ordered the combined Chart Set selector from newest to oldest across Report and standalone Chart Sets, and automatically select the newest available set regardless of its type.
- Allowed a CDR uploaded in a multi-file Workspace batch to select a VFUK or 3UK Vendor Mapping from the same batch; mappings are queued and processed before their dependent CDRs, while the upload dialog now distinguishes file transfer from the continuing background processing queue.
- Prefixed generated PowerPoint report names and their dedicated output folders with the creation timestamp for chronological filesystem ordering.
- Kept Reports and Charts job action buttons on a single compact row while fitting the tables to their available width without horizontal scrolling; preserved readable minimum widths for ID, Generated by, Date, Tech, Type and Progress, and gave Slides, Charts and Datasets additional space, while wrapping other long metadata within its cells and keeping the compact mobile card layout unchanged.
- Allowed PowerPoint Report and Chart Set jobs to run from any non-empty combination of Data, Voice and Speech CDRs, rendering an explicit unavailable-source placeholder for chart types without a selected CDR; added synchronized Tech, Type, Template and Scope filters to the Charts Panel selector and combined Jobs table, with Template choices retaining their registered capitalization and compact-phone job cards updating to the first matching page.
- Split Slides Templates Editor controls into non-fixed per-chart actions and block-level Slide Actions, with move, insert and delete controls consistently grouped on the first action row, constrained chart reordering, whole-slide movement/insertion/deletion, in-place duplication and chart/slide copying to a selected destination; selected dropdown values stay at the top and unsaved values and edited-cell highlighting survive table rebuilds.
- Refined workspace-owned Auto-calculated Fields management from Workspace, Slides Templates Editor and Chart Preview: the Chart Preview action now sits immediately beside Close, its wider dialog aligns its fields, compacts redundant case-only field aliases, documents case-insensitive matching, and uses a compact multi-choice Available for popover that closes after pointer exit or an outside interaction. The manager separates its Add action from the definition list, removes the editor Cancel action, provides a grey Close action beside Add or Save, keeps delete confirmations above the manager, replaces the minus deletion glyph with a cross, and adds distinct duplicate and purple download actions. Every save requires confirmation before rematerializing applicable CDR tables, and a rename also updates every Slides Template reference to that field.
- Reworked the Workspace Auto-calculated Fields panel with equal-width import and bounded, scrollable definition subpanels after Datasets, Field and Applied to column labels, compact `Edit` and green `Export` actions, a Choose JSON filename display, a flexible full-width filename field, and an Import action aligned in one row with a distinct filename background. The green `Re-materialize auto-calculate fields` action sits above both subpanels, to the right of the introductory description, combines its label with a refresh SVG, confirms the operation and runs it in the background. Imports confirm the operation and warn before overwriting existing fields, listing each overwritten field on its own bold bullet line. The Materialization status panel and each matching combined CDR row now share the exact task progress, with queued work explicitly at 0% rather than a synthetic 5%; unrelated combined CDR types no longer appear to recalculate. The Datasets panel now lists existing combined CDR tables at the bottom as `CDR-Data (combined)`, `CDR-Voice (combined)` or `CDR-Speech (combined)`, includes their row counts and Updated date, participates in the matching CDR type filter, and provides a Preview dataset action followed by a confirmed circular background Recreate action. Recreating a CDR type already in progress cooperatively stops its existing task before queuing its replacement, while non-critical audit-log contention cannot turn an already queued recreation into a browser error. A combined table whose rows differ from the sum of its ready individual CDRs shows an orange `Missing Rows` status; each Preview verifies it again and warns that it can be recreated from Workspace > Datasets before allowing the incomplete table to open. Combined previews reuse the individual CDR preview template, filters and column colours, open in their own tab, and recreation updates both the combined-row status/progress and Materialization status with the live percentage; it also recovers inconsistent empty individual row stores from their source workbooks, validates every contributed row count and refreshes Workspace after completion. The management and slide re-enumeration buttons use the shared yellow gradient.
- Restricted Result Group, Call Family, Test Family and other Auto-calculated Fields to their applicable individual and combined CDR tables, removed stale out-of-scope calculated columns during rematerialization, highlighted generated columns pastel red in CDR Preview, exposed them only in applicable dataset-field selectors without delaying Workspace rendering, and migrated and removed former per-template JSON sidecars.
- Added Auto-calculated Fields export, import and transfer as portable ZIP packages through Import / Export / Transfer, with multi-workspace destination selection on manual import and incoming transfer acceptance, preselecting the source workspace by name when it exists and leaving other destinations optional.
